💬9.18 外企职场1400句

未学 归档
点行首 🔊 朗读单行；英文行用英文语音，中文行用中文语音
1400 sentences commonly used in foreign companies
30岁 职场人学英语：
每天1小时，3个月告别哑巴英语
外企职场交流常用1400句
会议启动开场
Good morning/afternoon/evening, everyone. Thank you for joining this meeting.
（大家早上 / 下午 / 晚上好，感谢各位参加本次会议。）
Let's get started. First of all, I’d like to welcome everyone to this meeting.
（我们开始吧。首先，欢迎大家参加本次会议。）
I’m [Your Name], and I’ll be chairing this meeting today.
（我是 [你的名字]，今天由我主持本次会议。）
Before we begin, let’s go over the agenda briefly.
（在开始之前，我们先简要过一下议程。）
The main purpose of today’s meeting is to discuss [topic].
（今天会议的主要目的是讨论 [主题]。）
We have limited time, so please make sure your comments are concise.
（我们时间有限，所以请确保发言简明扼要。）
Could everyone please introduce themselves briefly?
（请各位简单自我介绍一下好吗？）
We’ll start with a quick update on the current situation.
（我们先快速了解一下当前情况。）
This meeting is being recorded for future reference.
（本次会议将进行录音，以供后续参考。）
If you have any questions during the meeting, feel free to raise your hand.
（会议期间如有任何问题，请随时举手提问。）
Welcome to today's cross-departmental meeting. We're here to align on the new project.
（欢迎参加今天的跨部门会议，我们此次是为了就新项目达成共识。）
First, let me express my gratitude to those who took time out of their busy schedules to attend.
（首先，感谢各位在百忙之中抽出时间参会。）
As the meeting host, I’ll keep track of time to ensure we cover all agenda items.
（作为会议主持人，我会把控时间，确保我们覆盖所有议程事项。）
Before diving into the topics, let’s confirm if everyone has received the meeting materials.
（在深入讨论议题之前，我们确认一下大家是否都已收到会议资料。）
Today’s meeting will focus on three key aspects: project progress, problem-solving, and next-step planning.
（今天的会议将围绕三个重点展开：项目进展、问题解决和后续计划制定。）
To make our discussion more efficient, please stay focused on the current agenda item.
（为了提高讨论效率，请大家聚焦当前的议程事项。）
If you’re joining remotely, please let us know if you have any connection issues.
（如果是远程参会，若有连接问题，请告知我们。）
Let’s take a minute to introduce any new colleagues who are attending the meeting for the first time.
（我们花一分钟时间，为首次参会的新同事做个介绍。）
The meeting minutes will be sent to everyone via email within 24 hours after the meeting.
（会议纪要将在会后 24 小时内通过邮件发送给大家。）
Our goal today is to reach a clear consensus on the matters we discuss.
（我们今天的目标是就讨论的事项达成明确共识。）
Good morning, team. Thanks for being here bright and early. Let’s make this meeting productive.
（早上好，团队成员们。感谢大家早早到场，让我们让这次会议富有成效。）
Before we start, I’d like to mention that we have a guest from the [Department Name] joining us today.
（开始之前，我想提一下，今天有一位来自 [部门名称] 的嘉宾加入我们。）
Let’s quickly go through the meeting rules: no interrupting others, keep comments relevant, and respect different opinions.
（我们快速过一下会议规则：不打断他人发言、发言紧扣主题、尊重不同意见。）
The main objective of this meeting is to review the previous stage’s work and set goals for the next stage.
（本次会议的主要目的是回顾上一阶段工作，并制定下一阶段目标。）
If you have any supplementary materials related to the agenda, please share them in the chat box now.
（如果大家有与议程相关的补充材料，请现在在聊天框中分享。）
Welcome everyone. Today, we’ll mainly discuss how to address the challenges we’re facing in the current project.
（欢迎大家，今天我们主要讨论如何解决当前项目中遇到的挑战。）
First, let’s hear a brief report from [Name] on the progress of the [Project Name] so far.
（首先，我们听听 [姓名] 关于 [项目名称] 到目前为止的进展简要汇报。）
We have a tight schedule today, so each agenda item will be allocated a specific amount of time.
（我们今天日程紧张，每个议程事项都会分配特定的时间。）
For those who couldn’t attend the last meeting, I’ll give a two-minute recap before we proceed.
（对于上次未能参会的同事，在我们继续之前，我会用两分钟做个简要回顾。）
The focus of today’s meeting is to collect everyone’s opinions on the new policy and make necessary adjustments.
（今天会议的重点是收集大家对新政策的意见，并进行必要调整。）
Good afternoon, everyone. I hope your day is going well. Let’s kick off today’s meeting with the first agenda item.
（大家下午好，希望大家今天一切顺利。让我们从第一个议程事项开始今天的会议。）
Before we begin the formal discussion, let’s confirm the attendance to ensure all key stakeholders are present.
（在正式讨论开始前，我们确认一下出席情况，确保所有关键相关方都已到场。）
I’d like to remind everyone that we need to make decisions on [Topic] today, so please come prepared with your thoughts.
（我想提醒大家，今天我们需要就 [主题] 做出决策，请大家准备好自己的想法。）
Today’s meeting will be divided into three parts: presentation, discussion, and conclusion. Let’s start with the presentation.
（今天的会议分为三个部分：汇报、讨论和总结。我们从汇报开始。）
Welcome to the weekly project meeting. Let’s first review what we accomplished last week.
（欢迎参加每周项目会议，我们先回顾一下上周完成的工作。）
Before diving into the details, let’s clarify the overall direction we’re heading in for the current task.
（在深入细节之前，我们先明确当前任务的总体方向。）
If you have any questions about the agenda, please ask now, and we’ll clarify them before moving forward.
（如果大家对议程有任何疑问，请现在提出，我们在继续之前会进行解答。）
Today, we have invited [Expert Name] to provide professional advice on [Field]. Let’s welcome them first.
（今天，我们邀请了 [专家姓名] 就 [领域] 提供专业建议，首先让我们欢迎他们。）
The meeting will last about 90 minutes. Let’s make sure we use every minute effectively.
（会议大约持续 90 分钟，让我们确保有效利用每一分钟。）
First, let’s take a moment to acknowledge the hard work of the team in the past month, which has laid a good foundation for our current work.
（首先，我们花一点时间肯定团队在过去一个月的辛勤工作，这为我们当前的工作奠定了良好基础。）
Good morning, everyone. Today’s meeting is crucial for the smooth progress of the project, so let’s stay focused.
（大家早上好，今天的会议对项目的顺利推进至关重要，所以请大家保持专注。）
Before we start, I’d like to share a few updates on the company’s recent developments that are relevant to our discussion today.
（开始之前，我想分享一些公司近期动态，这些动态与我们今天的讨论相关。）
Let’s begin by introducing the background of the [Issue] we’re going to discuss today, so everyone has a clear understanding.
（我们先介绍一下今天要讨论的 [问题] 的背景，让大家有清晰的了解。）
For remote participants, please ensure your microphone is muted when you’re not speaking to avoid background noise.
（远程参会的同事，在不发言时请确保麦克风静音，以避免背景噪音。）
Today’s meeting aims to resolve the outstanding issues from the previous discussion and confirm the next action steps.
（今天的会议旨在解决上次讨论遗留的问题，并确认后续行动步骤。）
First, let’s hear from [Name] who will explain the reasons for the recent changes in the project schedule.
（首先，我们听听 [姓名] 的发言，他将解释项目日程近期变动的原因。）
We have a lot to cover today, so let’s avoid going off on tangents and stay focused on the agenda.
（我们今天有很多内容要讨论，所以请大家避免跑题，聚焦议程。）
Before the formal meeting starts, let’s do a quick test of the audio and video to ensure everything is working properly.
（正式会议开始前，我们快速测试一下音视频，确保一切正常。）
Welcome everyone. Today, we’ll discuss how to optimize the current workflow to improve work efficiency.
（欢迎大家，今天我们将讨论如何优化当前工作流程，以提高工作效率。）
First, I’d like to thank [Name] for organizing this meeting and preparing all the relevant materials.
（首先，我要感谢 [姓名] 组织这次会议并准备了所有相关资料。）
议程推进执行
Now, let’s move on to the first item on the agenda: [topic].
（现在，我们进入议程的第一项：[主题]。）
Could [Name] please present the progress on [project]?
（请 [名字] 介绍一下 [项目] 的进展情况好吗？）
Thank you for the presentation. Now, let’s open the floor for discussion.
（感谢你的汇报。现在，我们开始自由讨论。）
Next on the agenda is [topic], and I’d like to hear everyone’s thoughts on it.
（议程的下一项是 [主题]，我想听听大家对此的看法。）
Let’s take a look at the data presented in this slide.
（我们来看一下这张幻灯片中的数据。）
According to the agenda, we should be discussing [topic] at this point.
（根据议程，我们现在应该讨论 [主题]。）
We seem to be running behind schedule. Let’s try to speed up.
（我们似乎进度落后了，尽量加快点速度。）
I’d like to summarize what we’ve discussed so far.
（我想总结一下到目前为止我们讨论的内容。）
Moving on to the next agenda item, which is [topic].
（继续下一项议程，即 [主题]。）
Let’s allocate 10 minutes for this topic to ensure we cover everything.
（我们给这个议题分配 10 分钟时间，确保讨论全面。）
Now that we’ve finished discussing the first agenda item, let’s move on to the second one: [Topic Name].
（既然我们已经讨论完第一个议程事项，现在进入第二个：[议题名称]。）
[Name], could you please give a detailed introduction to the current status of the [Project Name] as the next step in the agenda?
（[姓名]，作为议程的下一步，请你详细介绍一下 [项目名称] 的当前状况好吗？）
After [Name]’s presentation, we’ll have 15 minutes for questions and discussions to deepen our understanding of this agenda item.
（在 [姓名] 汇报后，我们将有 15 分钟的提问和讨论时间，以加深对这个议程事项的理解。）
The next agenda item is about [Topic], and I hope everyone can put forward practical suggestions based on their work experience.
（下一个议程事项是关于 [主题] 的，希望大家能结合自身工作经验提出切实可行的建议。）
Let’s focus on the key points of this agenda item. First, let’s clarify the core problems we need to solve.
（我们聚焦这个议程事项的重点，首先明确我们需要解决的核心问题。）
According to the original agenda, we should now start discussing the budget allocation for the next quarter. Let’s begin.
（根据原定议程，我们现在应该开始讨论下一季度的预算分配问题，我们开始吧。）
We’ve spent a little more time on the previous agenda item, so we need to reduce the discussion time for this one to 8 minutes to catch up.
（我们在上一个议程事项上花费了稍多时间，所以这个事项的讨论时间需要缩减到 8 分钟，以赶上进度。）
Before moving on to the next agenda item, let me briefly recap the key conclusions we reached on the current one.
（在进入下一个议程事项之前，我简要回顾一下我们在当前事项上达成的关键结论。）
The next agenda item is crucial for the project’s subsequent development, so let’s listen carefully to [Name]’s analysis.
（下一个议程事项对项目后续发展至关重要，所以请大家认真听取 [姓名] 的分析。）
Let’s set a clear time limit for this agenda item—we’ll finish the discussion in 12 minutes and then move on.
（我们为这个议程事项设定一个明确的时间限制，12 分钟内完成讨论，然后进入下一项。）
Now, let’s transition to the third agenda item: the optimization plan for the customer service process.
（现在，我们过渡到第三个议程事项：客户服务流程优化方案。）
[Name], as the person in charge of the [Department Name], could you lead the discussion on the next agenda item?
（[姓名]，作为 [部门名称] 的负责人，你能主导下一个议程事项的讨论吗？）
After the discussion on this agenda item, we need to confirm the responsible person and the completion time for each action.
（这个议程事项讨论结束后，我们需要确定各项行动的负责人和完成时间。）
The next agenda item involves cross-departmental collaboration, so I hope representatives from each relevant department can actively participate in the discussion.
（下一个议程事项涉及跨部门协作，希望各相关部门的代表能积极参与讨论。）
Let’s first review the background information of this agenda item to ensure everyone has the same understanding before the discussion.
（在讨论之前，我们先回顾一下这个议程事项的背景信息，确保大家有一致的认知。）
According to the adjusted agenda, we’ll first discuss the product launch plan and then move on to the marketing strategy.
（根据调整后的议程，我们先讨论产品发布计划，然后再进行营销策略的讨论。）
We’re a bit behind schedule, so let’s skip the unnecessary details for now and focus on the core content of this agenda item.
（我们进度有些落后，所以现在先跳过不必要的细节，聚焦这个议程事项的核心内容。）
Before moving to the next agenda item, is there anyone who hasn’t had a chance to express their views on the current one?
（在进入下一个议程事项之前，还有谁在当前事项上没有机会发表看法吗？）
The next agenda item is about addressing the problems encountered in the recent project implementation. Let’s first hear about the specific situations from each team.
（下一个议程事项是解决近期项目执行中遇到的问题，我们先听听各团队的具体情况。）
Let’s allocate time reasonably for this agenda item: 5 minutes for the introduction, 10 minutes for discussion, and 5 minutes for summarizing.
（我们为这个议程事项合理分配时间：介绍 5 分钟、讨论 10 分钟、总结 5 分钟。）
Now, let’s move on to the fourth agenda item: the training plan for new employees in the second half of the year.
（现在，我们进入第四个议程事项：下半年新员工培训计划。）
[Name], could you please present the preliminary plan for this agenda item first, and then we’ll discuss and revise it together?
（[姓名]，你能先就这个议程事项展示初步方案，然后我们一起讨论修改好吗？）
After discussing this agenda item, we need to form a written document to record the key points and action steps.
（这个议程事项讨论结束后，我们需要形成一份书面文件，记录要点和行动步骤。）
The next agenda item is related to the company’s long-term development strategy, so everyone should think from a long-term perspective when putting forward opinions.
（下一个议程事项与公司长期发展战略相关，所以大家在提出意见时要从长远角度考虑。）
Let’s first confirm whether everyone has understood the content of this agenda item. If there’s any confusion, please raise your hand and ask.
（我们先确认一下大家是否都理解这个议程事项的内容，如有疑问，请举手提问。）
According to the agenda, we should now discuss the cooperation plan with [Partner Company Name]. Let’s start with the preliminary communication results.
（根据议程，我们现在应该讨论与 [合作公司名称] 的合作方案，我们从初步沟通结果开始谈起。）
We need to speed up the progress of the agenda. For this item, let’s ask each person to express their core views in no more than 2 minutes.
（我们需要加快议程进度，对于这个事项，每个人的核心观点表达控制在 2 分钟以内。）
Before moving on to the next agenda item, let’s vote on the key proposal of the current item to reach a clear decision.
（在进入下一个议程事项之前，我们对当前事项的关键提案进行投票，以达成明确决策。）
The next agenda item is about improving the team’s work efficiency. Let’s focus on finding practical ways to solve the existing problems.
（下一个议程事项是关于提高团队工作效率的，我们重点寻找解决现有问题的切实方法。）
Let’s arrange the discussion order of this agenda item according to the departments. First, the [Department 1] will share their views, then the [Department 2], and so on.
（我们按照部门顺序安排这个议程事项的讨论顺序，首先是 [部门 1] 分享看法，然后是 [部门 2]，以此类推。）
Now, let’s shift our focus to the fifth agenda item: the risk assessment and prevention plan for the new project.
（现在，我们将焦点转向第五个议程事项：新项目的风险评估与防范方案。）
[Name], could you please update us on the latest progress related to this agenda item first, so we can have a more targeted discussion?
（[姓名]，你能先更新一下与这个议程事项相关的最新进展，以便我们进行更有针对性的讨论吗？）
After the discussion on this agenda item, we need to report the results to the senior management, so the conclusions should be clear and feasible.
（这个议程事项讨论结束后，我们需要向高层管理层汇报结果，所以结论要清晰可行。）
The next agenda item involves sensitive information, so please keep the discussion content confidential and not disclose it to external personnel.
（下一个议程事项涉及敏感信息，请大家对讨论内容保密，不向外部人员透露。）
Let’s first list the key issues of this agenda item on the whiteboard, and then discuss them one by one to ensure we don’t miss anything.
（我们先把这个议程事项的关键问题列在白板上，然后逐一讨论，确保不遗漏任何一点。）
According to the agenda adjustment, we’ll temporarily put aside the original sixth item and advance the discussion on the seventh item: [Topic Name].
（根据议程调整，我们暂时搁置原定的第六项，提前进行第七项 [议题名称] 的讨论。）
We’re running out of time, so for this agenda item, let’s directly focus on the solutions rather than dwelling on the problems themselves.
（时间不多了，所以这个议程事项我们直接聚焦解决方案，而不是纠结于问题本身。）
Before moving to the next agenda item, let’s confirm whether all the problems raised in the current item have been properly addressed.
（在进入下一个议程事项之前，我们确认一下当前事项中提出的所有问题是否都已妥善解决。）
The next agenda item is about the performance evaluation system reform. We hope everyone can put forward constructive opinions based on their actual work experience.
（下一个议程事项是关于绩效考核制度改革的，希望大家结合实际工作经验提出建设性意见。）
Let’s summarize the discussion of this agenda item in three sentences: first, the core problems identified; second, the proposed solutions; third, the next action steps.
（我们用三句话总结这个议程事项的讨论：一是明确的核心问题，二是提出的解决方案，三是后续的行动步骤。）
议题讨论发言
I’d like to share my opinion on this matter.
（我想就此事分享一下我的看法。）
From my perspective, [point of view].
（在我看来，[观点]。）
I completely agree with what [Name] just said.
（我完全同意 [名字] 刚才所说的。）
I’m afraid I have a different opinion.
（恐怕我有不同的意见。）
Could you elaborate on that point?
（你能详细说明一下那一点吗？）
That’s an interesting idea, but have you considered [factor]?
（这个想法很有趣，但是你考虑过 [因素] 吗？）
I think we should take [suggestion] into account.
（我认为我们应该考虑 [建议]。）
Let me clarify my point.
（我澄清一下我的观点。）
Can we explore some alternative solutions?
（我们能探讨一些其他的解决方案吗？）
I’d like to raise a concern about [issue].
（我想就 [问题] 提出一个担忧。）
What’s your take on [topic]?
（你对 [主题] 有什么看法？）
I believe we need to focus on [aspect] to solve this problem.
（我认为我们需要关注 [方面] 来解决这个问题。）
Do you have any data to support your claim?
（你有数据来支持你的观点吗？）
Let’s look at this from a different angle.
（我们从另一个角度来看这个问题。）
I’m not sure if that approach is feasible.
（我不确定那种方法是否可行。）
That’s a valid point, but we also need to consider [other factor].
（这是个有道理的观点，但我们也需要考虑 [其他因素]。）
Could you provide more details about [plan]?
（你能提供关于 [计划] 的更多细节吗？）
I’d like to add something to what [Name] said.
（我想补充一下 [名字] 所说的内容。）
Let’s brainstorm some ideas to improve [situation].
（我们集思广益，想些办法改善 [情况]。）
Is there anyone else who wants to share their thoughts?
（还有其他人想分享一下想法吗？）
Based on my experience in handling similar projects, I think we should prioritize [Task] first.
（根据我处理类似项目的经验，我认为我们应该先优先处理 [任务]。）
From the perspective of cost control, the solution proposed earlier may have some hidden expenses that we haven’t considered.
（从成本控制的角度来看，之前提出的方案可能存在一些我们没有考虑到的隐性支出。）
I fully agree with [Name]’s view on the importance of customer satisfaction, and I think we can further improve it by optimizing the after-sales service process.
（我完全同意 [姓名] 关于客户满意度重要性的观点，而且我认为我们可以通过优化售后服务流程来进一步提升它。）
I have a different opinion on the timeline of the project. I think we need to extend it by two weeks to ensure the quality of the deliverables.
（关于项目的时间节点，我有不同的看法，我认为我们需要延长两周，以确保交付成果的质量。）
Could you explain in more detail how the new marketing strategy will be implemented in the target market? I’m a bit confused about the specific steps.
（你能更详细地解释一下新的营销策略将如何在目标市场实施吗？我对具体步骤有点困惑。）
Your idea of expanding the product line is very good, but have you considered the impact it will have on our current production capacity?
（你提出的扩大产品线的想法很好，但你考虑过它对我们当前产能的影响吗？）
I think we should take the feedback from the front-line sales staff into account, as they have the most direct contact with customers and know their real needs best.
（我认为我们应该考虑一线销售人员的反馈，因为他们与客户接触最直接，最了解客户的真实需求。）
Let me rephrase my point to avoid misunderstanding: what I mean is that we should first conduct a small-scale test before promoting the new policy on a large scale.
（我重新表述一下我的观点，以避免误解：我的意思是在大规模推广新政策之前，我们应该先进行小规模测试。）
Instead of sticking to the current single solution, can we explore other options, such as cooperating with a third-party agency to complete this task?
（除了坚持当前这一个解决方案，我们能探索其他选择吗，比如与第三方机构合作完成这项任务？）
I’m worried that the current communication method between departments will lead to information delays, which may affect the progress of the project.
（我担心当前部门之间的沟通方式会导致信息延迟，这可能会影响项目的进度。）
What do you think about adjusting the team’s work schedule to better adapt to the time difference of our overseas clients?
（为了更好地适应海外客户的时差，调整团队的工作时间安排，你怎么看？）
I believe that to solve the problem of low work efficiency, we need to focus on optimizing the internal approval process, which currently takes too much time.
（我认为要解决工作效率低的问题，我们需要重点优化内部审批流程，目前这个流程花费的时间太多了。）
Do you have any specific data to prove that the new product will be popular in the market? For example, market research reports or customer survey results.
（你有具体的数据来证明新产品会在市场上受欢迎吗？比如市场调研报告或者客户调查结果。）
Let’s look at this problem from the perspective of our competitors. How would they handle this situation, and what can we learn from them?
（我们从竞争对手的角度来看这个问题，他们会如何处理这种情况，我们能从中学到什么？）
I’m not sure if the current technical solution is feasible in the long run, because it may not be compatible with the new technologies we plan to introduce next year.
（我不确定当前的技术方案长期来看是否可行，因为它可能与我们明年计划引入的新技术不兼容。）
You made a very valid point about the importance of talent retention, but we also need to consider the company’s current budget constraints when formulating the retention plan.
（你提出的关于人才保留重要性的观点很有道理，但我们在制定保留计划时，还需要考虑公司当前的预算限制。）
Could you provide more details about the training content for the new employees? For example, what professional skills will be covered and who will be the trainers?
（你能提供更多关于新员工培训内容的细节吗？比如会涵盖哪些专业技能，培训师是谁？）
I’d like to add that in addition to the measures mentioned earlier, we can also set up an incentive mechanism to encourage employees to put forward more innovative ideas.
（我想补充的是，除了之前提到的措施，我们还可以建立激励机制，鼓励员工提出更多创新想法。）
Let’s have a brainstorming session to think about how to improve the company’s brand image. Everyone can freely put forward their ideas, no matter how bold they are.
（我们来一场头脑风暴，思考如何提升公司的品牌形象，每个人都可以自由提出想法，不管有多大胆。）
Is there anyone who hasn’t expressed their views on the cooperation plan with [Partner Company]? We hope to hear more different voices.
（关于与 [合作公司] 的合作方案，还有谁没有发表看法吗？我们希望听到更多不同的声音。）
From the perspective of project risk management, I think we should formulate a contingency plan for the possible delay of the supplier’s delivery.
（从项目风险管理的角度来看，我认为我们应该为供应商可能出现的交货延迟制定一个应急方案。）
I think the current marketing campaign lacks targeted promotion for young consumer groups, and we can adjust the advertising content to attract this group.
（我认为当前的营销活动对年轻消费群体缺乏针对性推广，我们可以调整广告内容来吸引这个群体。）
Do you have any suggestions on how to improve the accuracy of the financial forecast? The current forecast results often deviate from the actual situation.
（关于如何提高财务预测的准确性，你有什么建议吗？目前的预测结果经常与实际情况有偏差。）
Let’s look at this issue from the perspective of customer experience. What changes can we make to make customers more satisfied with our services?
（我们从客户体验的角度来看这个问题，我们能做出哪些改变，让客户对我们的服务更满意？）
I’m not sure if the current team structure is suitable for the new project. Maybe we need to adjust the personnel allocation according to the project’s needs.
（我不确定当前的团队结构是否适合新项目，或许我们需要根据项目需求调整人员配置。）
Your analysis of the market trend is very in-depth, but we also need to consider the impact of policy changes on the market, which may change the current trend.
（你对市场趋势的分析很深入，但我们还需要考虑政策变化对市场的影响，这可能会改变当前的趋势。）
Could you provide more details about the implementation steps of the cost-saving plan? For example, which departments will be involved and when it will start.
（你能提供更多关于成本节约计划实施步骤的细节吗？比如会涉及哪些部门，什么时候开始实施。）
I’d like to add that we should also pay attention to the employee’s work-life balance while pursuing work efficiency, which is conducive to improving employee loyalty.
（我想补充的是，我们在追求工作效率的同时，也应该关注员工的工作与生活平衡，这有利于提高员工的忠诚度。）
Let’s brainstorm some ideas to solve the problem of high employee turnover. We can consider aspects such as salary, career development, and working environment.
（我们集思广益，想些办法解决员工流失率高的问题，可以从薪资、职业发展、工作环境等方面考虑。）
Is there anyone else who wants to share their thoughts on the company’s development strategy for the next five years? Your opinions are very important for the final formulation of the strategy.
（还有其他人想分享对公司未来五年发展战略的看法吗？你们的意见对战略的最终制定非常重要。）
内容总结归纳
To sum up, our main points of discussion were [list main points].
（总结一下，我们讨论的主要内容是 [列出要点]。）
Let me recap what we’ve decided so far.
（我回顾一下到目前为止我们做出的决定。）
In conclusion, we agreed that [agreement].
（总之，我们达成共识，[共识内容]。）
I’ll summarize the key action items from this meeting.
（我将总结本次会议的关键行动事项。）
We’ve covered a lot today. Here’s a quick summary.
（我们今天讨论了很多内容，以下是一个简要总结。）
Based on our discussion, the next steps are [list steps].
（根据我们的讨论，接下来的步骤是 [列出步骤]。）
Let’s review the main takeaways from this meeting.
（我们回顾一下本次会议的主要收获。）
I’ll send out a summary email with all the details after the meeting.
（会议结束后，我会发送包含所有细节的总结邮件。）
To wrap things up, we need to focus on [key area].
（总结一下，我们需要关注 [关键领域]。）
The main outcomes of this meeting are [list outcomes].
（本次会议的主要成果是 [列出成果]。）
To sum up, today we focused on three main aspects of the project: the current progress, the existing problems, and the solutions to be implemented.
（总结一下，今天我们围绕项目的三个主要方面展开：当前进展、存在的问题以及将要实施的解决方案。）
Let me recap the key decisions we’ve made so far: first, we’ll adjust the project timeline by extending it by two weeks; second, we’ll increase the budget for the marketing campaign by 10%.
（我回顾一下到目前为止我们做出的关键决策：一是将项目时间线延长两周；二是将营销活动预算增加 10%。）
In conclusion, we all agreed that we should prioritize improving the quality of customer service, and we’ll formulate a specific improvement plan within a week.
（总之，我们一致同意应该优先提升客户服务质量，并在一周内制定具体的改进计划。）
Now, I’ll summarize the key action items from this meeting: [Name 1] is responsible for collecting customer feedback and submitting a report by Friday; [Name 2] will coordinate with the technical team to solve the system bug by next Monday.
（现在，我总结本次会议的关键行动事项：[姓名 1] 负责收集客户反馈，周五前提交报告；[姓名 2] 协调技术团队，下周一前解决系统漏洞。）
We’ve covered a wide range of topics today, from the company’s quarterly performance review to the planning of the next year’s work. Here’s a quick summary of the key points.
（我们今天讨论的话题很广泛，从公司季度业绩回顾到明年工作计划制定，以下是要点的简要总结。）
Based on our in-depth discussion, the next steps we need to take are as follows: first, hold a special meeting with the supplier to negotiate the delivery time; second, organize a training session for employees on the new system.
（根据我们的深入讨论，接下来需要采取的步骤如下：一是与供应商召开专项会议，协商交货时间；二是组织员工参加新系统培训课程。）
Let’s review the main takeaways from this meeting: we have a clearer understanding of the market demand, identified the key problems in the current work, and formulated preliminary solutions for them.
（我们回顾一下本次会议的主要收获：我们对市场需求有了更清晰的认识，明确了当前工作中的关键问题，并为此制定了初步的解决方案。）
After the meeting, I’ll send out a summary email that includes the meeting agenda, key discussion points, decisions made, and action items, so everyone can review it later.
（会议结束后，我会发送一封总结邮件，内容包括会议议程、关键讨论点、做出的决策以及行动事项，方便大家后续查阅。）
To wrap things up, in the next stage of work, we need to focus on the two key areas of cost control and efficiency improvement, and all departments should coordinate closely to achieve the set goals.
（总结一下，在接下来的工作阶段，我们需要聚焦成本控制和效率提升这两个关键领域，各部门要密切配合，实现既定目标。）
The main outcomes of this meeting are: first, we finalized the cooperation plan with [Partner Company]; second, we determined the promotion time of the new product; third, we clarified the division of responsibilities for the next project.
（本次会议的主要成果是：一是敲定了与 [合作公司] 的合作方案；二是确定了新产品的推广时间；三是明确了下一个项目的职责分工。）
To sum up, the core content of our discussion today revolves around how to solve the problem of low sales volume. We put forward many valuable suggestions, such as optimizing the sales channel and adjusting the product price.
（总结一下，我们今天讨论的核心内容围绕如何解决销售量低的问题展开，大家提出了很多有价值的建议，比如优化销售渠道、调整产品价格等。）
Let me recap the important information we’ve discussed so far: the customer’s demand for product customization is increasing, the production cycle needs to be shortened by half a month, and the quality inspection standards will be further improved.
（我回顾一下到目前为止我们讨论的重要信息：客户对产品定制的需求不断增加，生产周期需要缩短半个月，质量检测标准将进一步提高。）
In conclusion, after full discussion, we all agreed that the company should increase investment in research and development to enhance the competitiveness of products in the market.
（总之，经过充分讨论，我们一致同意公司应加大研发投入，以提升产品在市场中的竞争力。）
I’ll summarize the key action items that need to be completed in the near future: the marketing department should complete the promotional material design by the end of this month; the production department needs to adjust the production plan according to the new order quantity.
（我总结一下近期需要完成的关键行动事项：市场部要在本月底完成宣传物料设计；生产部需要根据新的订单量调整生产计划。）
We’ve discussed a lot of important issues today, and here’s a quick summary to help everyone sort out their thoughts: we analyzed the reasons for the project delay, discussed the countermeasures, and confirmed the responsible persons for each measure.
（我们今天讨论了很多重要问题，以下是简要总结，帮助大家梳理思路：我们分析了项目延误的原因，讨论了应对措施，并确定了各项措施的负责人。）
Based on our discussion, the next steps for the team are: first, conduct a comprehensive investigation of the market to understand the latest market trends; second, hold a brainstorming session to collect innovative ideas for product improvement.
（根据我们的讨论，团队接下来的步骤是：一是对市场进行全面调研，了解最新的市场趋势；二是召开头脑风暴会议，收集产品改进的创新想法。）

After the meeting, I’ll sort out all the discussion content and send a detailed summary email to everyone, which will include the specific content of each agenda item and the corresponding decisions.

（总结一下，今天的讨论聚焦于公司内部管理流程的优化，我们发现了审批流程繁琐、岗位职责不明确等问题，并提出了相应的改进建议。）
Let me recap the key points we’ve discussed so far to ensure everyone has the same understanding: the project will be divided into three phases, each phase has clear goals and time limits, and the technical team will provide full support throughout the process.
（我回顾一下到目前为止我们讨论的要点，确保大家有一致的认识：项目将分为三个阶段，每个阶段都有明确的目标和时限，技术团队将在整个过程中提供全力支持。）
In conclusion, after in-depth communication, we all agreed that we should adjust the company’s marketing strategy according to the changes in the market environment, and focus on the promotion of online marketing channels.

会议时间管管控
We’ve got 15 minutes left. Let’s make the most of it.
（我们还剩 15 分钟，充分利用时间吧。）
We’re running out of time. Let’s move on.
（时间不多了，我们继续下一项。）
Could you please keep your comments within 2 minutes?
（请将发言控制在 2 分钟以内好吗？）
We’ve spent too much time on this topic. Let’s shift gears.
（我们在这个话题上花的时间太多了，换个话题吧。）
Let’s allocate the remaining time evenly among the remaining topics.
（我们把剩余时间平均分配给剩下的议题。）
We’re behind schedule. We need to speed up.
（我们进度落后了，需要加快速度。）
Please be concise as we have limited time.
（由于时间有限，请言简意赅。）
We still have 10 minutes for questions.
（我们还有 10 分钟时间用于提问。）
Let’s finish this discussion in the next 5 minutes.
（我们在接下来 5 分钟内结束这个讨论。）
Time’s up. Let’s move on to the next item.
（时间到了，我们进入下一项。）
We only have 20 minutes left for the entire meeting, so we need to focus on the most important agenda items first.
（整个会议我们只剩 20 分钟了，所以需要先聚焦最重要的议程事项。）
The clock is ticking, and we’re running out of time to discuss all the planned topics, so let’s skip the less critical content.
（时间在流逝，我们已经没有足够时间讨论所有计划的话题了，所以跳过不太关键的内容吧。）
To ensure we don’t exceed the meeting time, could you please keep your comments within 2 minutes when sharing your views on this topic?
（为了确保不超过会议时长，在就这个话题分享看法时，请将发言控制在 2 分钟以内好吗？）
We’ve spent nearly 30 minutes on this topic, which is more than the planned 15 minutes. Let’s shift gears and start discussing the next agenda item.
（我们在这个话题上已经花了近 30 分钟，超出了计划的 15 分钟，换个话题，开始讨论下一个议程事项吧。）
There are three topics left to discuss, and we have 45 minutes remaining. Let’s allocate 15 minutes to each topic to ensure we can cover all of them.
（还剩三个话题要讨论，我们还有 45 分钟，每个话题分配 15 分钟，确保能全部覆盖。）
We’re already 10 minutes behind the scheduled meeting progress. We need to speed up the discussion pace and avoid unnecessary digressions.
（我们已经比计划的会议进度落后了 10 分钟，需要加快讨论节奏，避免不必要的偏离主题。）
As we have limited time for this meeting, please be concise when introducing your work progress, focusing only on the key points and results.
（由于本次会议时间有限，在介绍工作进展时请言简意赅，只聚焦要点和成果。）
We still have 10 minutes left for the question-and-answer session. Please raise your hand if you have any questions, and we’ll answer them one by one.
（提问环节我们还有 10 分钟，如有问题请举手，我们会逐一解答。）
Let’s set a clear goal: to finish this discussion on the project budget in the next 5 minutes, so we can have enough time for the subsequent summary.
（我们设定一个明确目标：接下来 5 分钟内结束关于项目预算的讨论，这样后续总结就有足够时间。）
Time’s up for the current agenda item. Even if there are still some minor issues to discuss, we’ll put them aside for now and move on to the next item.
（当前议程事项时间到了，即使还有一些小问题要讨论，我们也先搁置，进入下一项。）
We have only 15 minutes left before the meeting ends, so let’s focus on finalizing the key action items instead of continuing to discuss the causes of the problems.
（会议结束前我们只剩 15 分钟了，所以聚焦敲定关键行动事项，而不是继续讨论问题原因。）
We’re running out of time, and we still haven’t discussed the most important topic of the meeting. Let’s stop the current discussion and directly move on to that key topic.
（时间不多了，我们还没讨论会议最重要的话题，停止当前讨论，直接进入那个关键话题。）
When expressing your opinions on the marketing plan, could you please keep it within 2 minutes? This way, everyone can have a chance to speak within the limited time.
（在就营销计划发表意见时，请控制在 2 分钟以内好吗？这样在有限时间里每个人都能有发言机会。）
We’ve spent too much time debating the details of this plan. Let’s shift gears and discuss whether the overall direction of the plan is feasible first.
（我们在这个计划的细节上争论太久了，换个方向，先讨论计划的整体方向是否可行。）
There are two topics left, and we have 30 minutes left. Let’s allocate 20 minutes to the first topic, which is more complex, and 10 minutes to the second one.
（还剩两个话题，我们还有 30 分钟，第一个话题较复杂，分配 20 分钟，第二个分配 10 分钟。）
We’re significantly behind schedule because of the prolonged discussion on the previous topic. We need to speed up and even shorten the time for the next two topics by 5 minutes each.
（由于上一个话题讨论时间过长，我们进度严重落后，需要加快速度，甚至将接下来两个话题的时间各缩短 5 分钟。）
Please be concise when reporting the project progress. We don’t have much time, so just tell us the key achievements, existing problems, and next-step plans.
（汇报项目进展时请言简意赅，时间不多了，只需告知关键成果、存在问题和下一步计划。）
We still have 10 minutes for questions. To make the most of this time, please ask targeted questions instead of raising irrelevant ones.
（提问环节还有 10 分钟，为充分利用时间，请提出有针对性的问题，不要提无关问题。）
Let’s make a concerted effort to finish this discussion on the cooperation plan in the next 5 minutes. We can discuss the minor details after the meeting if needed.
（我们共同努力，接下来 5 分钟内结束关于合作方案的讨论，如有需要，会后再讨论小细节。）
Time’s up for the discussion on the employee training plan. Let’s move on to the next agenda item, which is the review of the company’s quarterly performance.
（员工培训计划的讨论时间到了，进入下一个议程事项，即公司季度业绩回顾。）
We have only 25 minutes left for the entire meeting. Let’s first confirm the key decisions we need to make today and focus on discussing those.
（整个会议只剩 25 分钟了，先确认今天需要做出的关键决策，聚焦讨论这些内容。）
We’re running out of time, and we still have three agenda items left. Let’s merge the two similar items into one discussion to save time.
（时间不多了，还剩三个议程事项，把两个相似的事项合并讨论，以节省时间。）
When commenting on the product design plan, could you please keep your remarks within 2 minutes? This will help us ensure the meeting proceeds as scheduled.
（在对产品设计方案发表评论时，请将发言控制在 2 分钟以内好吗？这有助于我们确保会议按计划进行。）
We’ve spent over 20 minutes on this technical issue, which is beyond the planned time. Let’s shift gears and ask the technical team to provide a written report on this issue later.
（我们在这个技术问题上花了 20 多分钟，超出了计划时间，换个话题，让技术团队后续就这个问题提供书面报告。）
There are four topics left, and we have 60 minutes remaining. Let’s allocate 15 minutes to each topic on average, and the host will remind everyone when the time is almost up.
（还剩四个话题，我们还有 60 分钟，每个话题平均分配 15 分钟，时间快到时主持人会提醒大家。）
We’re 15 minutes behind schedule. To catch up, we need to reduce the discussion time for each subsequent agenda item by 3 minutes.
（我们进度落后了 15 分钟，为赶上进度，后续每个议程事项的讨论时间都要减少 3 分钟。）
Please be concise in your speech, especially when introducing the background of the problem. We already have a basic understanding, so we just need the key information.
（发言请言简意赅，尤其是介绍问题背景时，我们已有基本了解，只需关键信息。）
We still have 10 minutes for the question-and-answer session. Let’s prioritize the questions related to the implementation of the plan, as those are more urgent.
（提问环节还有 10 分钟，优先解答与计划实施相关的问题，这些问题更紧急。）
Let’s set a timer for 5 minutes. We’ll finish the discussion on the budget adjustment within this time and then start the meeting summary.
（我们设个 5 分钟的计时器，在这段时间内结束预算调整的讨论，然后开始会议总结。）
Time’s up. Even if some colleagues haven’t finished their speeches, we’ll ask them to send their views to everyone via email after the meeting. Now, let’s move on to the next item.
（时间到了，即使有些同事还没发言结束，我们请他们会后通过邮件将观点发给大家，现在进入下一项。）
We have only 20 minutes left, so let’s skip the detailed introduction of the previous work and directly discuss the problems we’re currently facing and the solutions.
（我们只剩 20 分钟了，跳过前期工作的详细介绍，直接讨论当前面临的问题和解决方案。）
We’re running out of time, and the most important thing today is to reach a consensus on the project launch time. Let’s focus all our time and energy on this issue.
（时间不多了，今天最重要的事是就项目启动时间达成共识，把所有时间和精力都聚焦在这个问题上。）
When sharing your experience in project management, could you please keep it within 2 minutes? We need to leave enough time for other colleagues to share.
（在分享项目管理经验时，请控制在 2 分钟以内好吗？我们需要给其他同事留出足够的分享时间。）
We’ve spent too much time on this trivial matter. Let’s shift gears and discuss the strategic issues that affect the company’s long-term development.
（我们在这件小事上花了太多时间，换个方向，讨论影响公司长期发展的战略问题。）
There are two topics left, and we have 35 minutes left. Let’s allocate 20 minutes to the first topic, which requires in-depth discussion, and 15 minutes to the second one for a brief review.
（还剩两个话题，我们还有 35 分钟，第一个话题需要深入讨论，分配 20 分钟，第二个话题简要回顾，分配 15 分钟。）
We’re 20 minutes behind schedule. We need to speed up the discussion and even cancel the 10-minute break we originally planned to ensure we finish all the agenda items.
（我们进度落后了 20 分钟，需要加快讨论速度，甚至取消原本计划的 10 分钟休息时间，确保完成所有议程事项。）
Please be concise when explaining the reasons for the work delay. We just need the key factors, not a detailed narrative of the entire process.
（解释工作延误原因时请言简意赅，只需关键因素，不用详细叙述整个过程。）
We still have 10 minutes for questions. To improve efficiency, please prepare your questions in advance and avoid thinking about them on the spot.
（提问环节还有 10 分钟，为提高效率，请提前准备好问题，不要当场思考。）
Let’s make a commitment to finish this discussion on the new policy implementation plan in the next 5 minutes. Any unresolved issues can be discussed in a follow-up meeting.
（我们承诺接下来 5 分钟内结束关于新政策实施计划的讨论，未解决的问题可以在后续会议中讨论。）
Time’s up for today’s meeting. We haven’t finished discussing all the agenda items, so we’ll arrange a special meeting next week to continue the discussion. Now, let’s conclude the meeting.
（今天会议时间到了，我们还没讨论完所有议程事项，下周安排专项会议继续讨论，现在会议结束。）
意见征集征询
What do you think about [proposal]?
（你对 [提议] 有什么看法？）
Does anyone have any suggestions?
（有人有什么建议吗？）
I’d like to hear your feedback on [plan].
（我想听听你对 [计划] 的反馈。）
Any thoughts on how we can improve [process]?
（关于如何改进 [流程]，大家有什么想法？）
Are there any objections to [decision]?
（对 [决定] 有任何异议吗？）
Do you agree with this approach?
（你同意这种方法吗？）
What’s your recommendation regarding [issue]?
（关于 [问题]，你的建议是什么？）
I’m open to any ideas or suggestions.
（我乐于接受任何想法或建议。）
Can anyone offer a different perspective?
（有人能提供不同的观点吗？）
Does anyone have anything to add?
（还有人要补充的吗？）
What do you think about the proposal to increase the marketing budget by 20% next quarter? Do you think it’s necessary?
（对于下季度将营销预算增加 20% 的提议，你怎么看？你认为有必要吗？）
We’re currently considering adjusting the team’s work schedule to better cooperate with overseas clients. Does anyone have any suggestions on the specific adjustment plan?
（我们目前在考虑调整团队工作时间，以便更好地与海外客户合作，关于具体的调整方案，有人有什么建议吗？）
I’ve drafted a preliminary plan for the new employee training. I’d like to hear your feedback on the training content, duration, and trainers.
（我已经起草了新员工培训的初步方案，想听听大家对培训内容、时长以及培训师的反馈。）
The current approval process for purchasing office supplies is relatively cumbersome. Any thoughts on how we can simplify this process to improve efficiency?
（当前办公用品采购的审批流程比较繁琐，关于如何简化这个流程以提高效率，大家有什么想法？）
We’ve decided to launch a new product line next month. Are there any objections to the proposed launch time and marketing strategy?
（我们决定下个月推出一条新产品线，对于拟定的推出时间和营销策略，有任何异议吗？）
We plan to adopt a new project management software to improve work efficiency. Do you agree with this approach, or do you have other software recommendations?
（我们计划采用一款新的项目管理软件来提高工作效率，你同意这种方法吗，或者你有其他软件推荐吗？）
Regarding the issue of frequent delays in the delivery of raw materials by suppliers, what’s your recommendation on how to solve this problem? Should we find alternative suppliers or strengthen communication with the current ones?
（关于供应商原材料交货频繁延迟的问题，你有什么解决建议？是寻找备选供应商还是加强与现有供应商的沟通？）
We’re going to organize a company-wide team-building activity next quarter. I’m open to any ideas or suggestions on the activity location, form, and time.
（下季度我们要组织一次全公司的团建活动，关于活动地点、形式和时间，我乐于接受任何想法和建议。）
Most of us have been discussing the problem from the perspective of cost control. Can anyone offer a different perspective, such as considering the impact on product quality or customer satisfaction?
（我们大多数人都从成本控制的角度讨论这个问题，有人能提供不同的视角吗，比如考虑对产品质量或客户满意度的影响？）
We’ve finished discussing the main content of the cooperation plan with [Partner Company]. Does anyone have anything to add, such as matters that need to be paid attention to in the subsequent implementation process?
（我们已经讨论完与 [合作公司] 合作方案的主要内容，还有人要补充吗，比如后续实施过程中需要注意的事项？）
What do you think about the idea of setting up a customer feedback collection platform to better understand customer needs? Do you think it will be effective?
（为了更好地了解客户需求，建立一个客户反馈收集平台，你怎么看这个想法？你认为会有效吗？）
We’re facing a shortage of technical personnel in the project team. Does anyone have any suggestions on how to solve this problem, such as internal transfer or external recruitment?
（项目团队目前面临技术人员短缺的问题，关于如何解决这个问题，有人有什么建议吗，比如内部调动还是外部招聘？）
I’ve formulated a preliminary plan for improving the company’s after-sales service. I’d like to hear your feedback on the service standards, response time, and complaint handling process.
（我制定了一份改善公司售后服务的初步计划，想听听大家对服务标准、响应时间以及投诉处理流程的反馈。）
The current employee performance evaluation system has some problems, such as unclear evaluation indicators. Any thoughts on how we can improve this system to make it more fair and reasonable?
（当前员工绩效考核制度存在一些问题，比如考核指标不明确，关于如何改进这个制度使其更公平合理，大家有什么想法？）
We plan to adjust the product price based on market changes. Are there any objections to the proposed price adjustment range and implementation time?
（我们计划根据市场变化调整产品价格，对于拟定的调价幅度和实施时间，有任何异议吗？）
We’re considering changing the current office location to a more convenient area. Do you agree with this approach, or do you think we should keep the current location?
（我们在考虑将当前办公地点搬到更便利的区域，你同意这种方法吗，或者你认为我们应该保留当前地点？）
Regarding the issue of low employee participation in company training activities, what’s your recommendation on how to increase participation? Should we adjust the training content or set up incentives?
（关于员工参与公司培训活动积极性低的问题，你有什么提高参与度的建议？是调整培训内容还是设立激励机制？）
We’re going to develop a new marketing campaign for the upcoming holiday. I’m open to any ideas or suggestions on the campaign theme, promotional methods, and target audience.
（ upcoming holiday, we're going to develop a new marketing campaign. I'm open to any ideas or suggestions on the campaign theme, promotional methods, and target audience.）
Most of the discussions so far have focused on the short-term benefits of the project. Can anyone offer a different perspective, such as analyzing the long-term impact on the company’s development?
（到目前为止，大部分讨论都聚焦于项目的短期利益，有人能提供不同的视角吗，比如分析对公司发展的长期影响？）
We’ve discussed the main measures to reduce production costs. Does anyone have anything to add, such as other cost-saving points we haven’t considered?
（我们已经讨论了降低生产成本的主要措施，还有人要补充吗，比如我们没有考虑到的其他成本节约点？）
What do you think about the proposal to establish a cross-departmental communication team to improve coordination between departments? Do you think it will help solve the current communication problems?
（关于成立跨部门沟通小组以改善部门间协作的提议，你怎么看？你认为这有助于解决当前的沟通问题吗？）
We’re currently short of funds for the project. Does anyone have any suggestions on how to raise funds, such as applying for a loan or adjusting the project budget?
（我们目前项目资金短缺，关于如何筹集资金，有人有什么建议吗，比如申请贷款还是调整项目预算？）
I’ve designed a new workflow for the sales department. I’d like to hear your feedback on the workflow steps, responsibility division, and potential problems.
（我为销售部门设计了一个新的工作流程，想听听大家对流程步骤、职责分工以及可能存在问题的反馈。）
The current method of storing company data has certain security risks. Any thoughts on how we can improve the data security protection measures, such as upgrading the security system or strengthening employee training?
（当前公司数据存储方式存在一定安全风险，关于如何改进数据安全保护措施，比如升级安全系统或加强员工培训，大家有什么想法？）
We plan to participate in an industry exhibition next year to promote our products. Are there any objections to the proposed exhibition time, location, and booth design?
（我们计划明年参加一个行业展会来推广产品，对于拟定的展会时间、地点以及展位设计，有任何异议吗？）
We’re considering introducing a flexible working system for some positions. Do you agree with this approach, and what do you think are the potential advantages and disadvantages?
（我们在考虑为部分岗位引入弹性工作制度，你同意这种方法吗，你认为可能存在哪些优缺点？）
Regarding the issue of slow progress in the R&D of new products, what’s your recommendation on how to accelerate the progress? Should we increase the R&D team or adjust the R&D plan?
（关于新产品研发进度缓慢的问题，你有什么加快进度的建议？是增加研发人员还是调整研发计划？）
We’re going to revise the company’s employee handbook. I’m open to any ideas or suggestions on the content that needs to be added, deleted, or modified.
（我们要修订公司的员工手册，关于需要新增、删除或修改的内容，我乐于接受任何想法和建议。）
Most colleagues believe that we should focus on expanding the domestic market first. Can anyone offer a different perspective, such as suggesting that we explore the international market simultaneously?
（大部分同事认为我们应先聚焦拓展国内市场，有人能提供不同的视角吗，比如建议同时开拓国际市场？）
We’ve discussed the main content of the employee incentive plan. Does anyone have anything to add, such as other incentive methods that can better motivate employees?
（我们已经讨论了员工激励计划的主要内容，还有人要补充吗，比如其他能更好激励员工的激励方式？）
What do you think about the idea of cooperating with a professional training institution to improve the professional skills of employees? Do you think it’s more effective than internal training?
（关于与专业培训机构合作以提升员工专业技能的想法，你怎么看？你认为这比内部培训更有效吗？）
We’re facing intense competition in the market. Does anyone have any suggestions on how to enhance the company’s competitive advantage, such as improving product quality or innovating service models?
（我们在市场中面临激烈的竞争，关于如何提升公司的竞争优势，有人有什么建议吗，比如提高产品质量或创新服务模式？）
I’ve formulated a preliminary plan for the company’s annual meeting. I’d like to hear your feedback on the meeting theme, agenda, and venue selection.
（我制定了公司年会的初步方案，想听听大家对会议主题、议程以及场地选择的反馈。）
The current method of customer relationship management is not very effective. Any thoughts on how we can improve it, such as establishing a customer classification management system or increasing customer follow-up frequency?
（当前客户关系管理方式效果不太理想，关于如何改进，比如建立客户分类管理制度或增加客户跟进频次，大家有什么想法？）
We plan to adjust the company’s organizational structure to adapt to the new development strategy. Are there any objections to the proposed adjustment plan and personnel allocation?
（我们计划调整公司组织结构以适应新的发展战略，对于拟定的调整方案和人员配置，有任何异议吗？）
We’re considering launching a customer loyalty program to improve customer retention. Do you agree with this approach, and what rewards do you think should be included in the program?
（我们在考虑推出客户忠诚度计划以提高客户留存率，你同意这种方法吗，你认为计划中应包含哪些奖励措施？）
Regarding the issue of high energy consumption in the production process, what’s your recommendation on how to reduce energy consumption? Should we upgrade equipment or optimize the production process?
（关于生产过程中能耗较高的问题，你有什么降低能耗的建议？是升级设备还是优化生产流程？）
We’re going to develop a new corporate culture promotion plan. I’m open to any ideas or suggestions on the promotion methods, content, and time arrangement.
（我们要制定新的企业文化推广计划，关于推广方式、内容以及时间安排，我乐于接受任何想法和建议。）
Most of the discussions have focused on the technical aspects of the project. Can anyone offer a different perspective, such as considering the impact on the company’s brand image or social responsibility?
（大部分讨论都聚焦于项目的技术层面，有人能提供不同的视角吗，比如考虑对公司品牌形象或社会责任的影响？）
We’ve discussed the main measures to improve customer satisfaction. Does anyone have anything to add, such as services we can provide to meet customer 潜在需求 (potential needs)?
（我们已经讨论了提高客户满意度的主要措施，还有人要补充吗，比如我们可以提供哪些服务来满足客户的潜在需求？）
观点认同表达
I totally agree.
（我完全同意。）
That’s a great point. I couldn’t agree more.
（说得太对了，我完全赞同。）
Exactly! That’s what I was thinking.
（正是！我也是这么想的。）
I’m on the same page as you.
（我和你想法一致。）
You’ve got a good point there.
（你说得很有道理。）
I concur with your opinion.
（我同意你的观点。）
That’s precisely what we need to do.
（这正是我们需要做的。）
I support this idea.
（我支持这个想法。）
You’re absolutely right.
（你说得完全正确。）
I think we’re in agreement on this.
（我认为我们在这一点上达成了共识。）
I totally agree with the proposal to optimize the customer service process; it will definitely improve customer satisfaction.

不同意见阐述
I’m sorry, but I have to disagree.
（抱歉，但我不同意。）
I see your point, but I have a different view.
（我明白你的观点，但我有不同看法。）
I’m not convinced by this proposal.
（我对这个提议不太信服。）
I’m afraid I can’t support this decision.
（恐怕我不能支持这个决定。）
That might work in theory, but in practice…
（这在理论上可行，但在实践中……）
I have some reservations about [plan].
（我对 [计划] 有些保留意见。）
I don’t think this is the best approach.
（我认为这不是最好的方法。）
While I understand your perspective, I still disagree.
（虽然我理解你的观点，但我还是不同意。）
I’m not sure if this will achieve our goals.
（我不确定这能否实现我们的目标。）
This solution seems to have some flaws.
（这个解决方案似乎存在一些缺陷。）
I’m sorry, but I have to disagree with the idea of launching the new product next month. The market research is not yet complete, and we don’t have a clear understanding of customer needs.
（抱歉，但我不同意下个月推出新产品的想法。市场调研还未完成，我们对客户需求没有清晰的了解。）
I see your point about increasing the marketing budget to boost sales, but I have a different view. The current sales decline is due to product quality issues, not insufficient promotion.
（我明白你为了提高销量而增加营销预算的观点，但我有不同看法。当前销量下滑是因为产品质量问题，而非推广不足。）
I’m not convinced by this proposal to relocate the office. The new location is too far from most employees’ homes, which will increase their commuting time and may affect work enthusiasm.
（我对搬迁办公地点的这个提议不太信服。新地点离大多数员工家太远，会增加他们的通勤时间，可能影响工作积极性。）
I’m afraid I can’t support this decision to cut the R&D budget. R&D is crucial for the company’s long-term development, and reducing investment will affect our product innovation capability.
（恐怕我不能支持削减研发预算的这个决定。研发对公司长期发展至关重要，减少投入会影响我们的产品创新能力。）
That might work in theory—hiring more temporary employees to solve the labor shortage—but in practice, temporary employees lack familiarity with the company’s processes and may reduce work efficiency.
（从理论上讲，招聘更多临时工来解决劳动力短缺问题是可行的，但在实践中，临时工对公司流程不熟悉，可能会降低工作效率。）
I have some reservations about the plan to adopt the new project management software. The software is too complex, and training employees to use it will take a lot of time and resources.
（我对采用新的项目管理软件的计划有些保留意见。这款软件过于复杂，培训员工使用会花费大量时间和资源。）
I don’t think this is the best approach to improve customer satisfaction—simply extending the after-sales service time. We should first solve the root cause of customer complaints, such as product defects.
（我认为单纯延长售后服务时间不是提高客户满意度的最好方法。我们应该先解决客户投诉的根本原因，比如产品缺陷问题。）
While I understand your perspective that we should prioritize short-term profits, I still disagree. Ignoring product quality for short-term profits will damage the company’s reputation in the long run.
（虽然我理解你优先考虑短期利润的观点，但我还是不同意。为了短期利润而忽视产品质量，长期来看会损害公司声誉。）
I’m not sure if this method of increasing employee workload to meet the project deadline will achieve our goals. It may lead to employee burnout and a decline in work quality.
（我不确定通过增加员工工作量来赶上项目截止日期的这种方法能否实现我们的目标。这可能会导致员工 burnout，进而使工作质量下降。）
This solution to solve the supplier delivery delay problem—only strengthening communication—seems to have some flaws. We also need to formulate penalty clauses in the contract to constrain the supplier’s behavior.
（仅通过加强沟通来解决供应商交货延迟问题的这个解决方案似乎存在一些缺陷。我们还需要在合同中制定惩罚条款，约束供应商的行为。）
I’m sorry, but I have to disagree with the idea of canceling the team-building activity. Team-building is important for improving team cohesion, and canceling it may affect team collaboration.
（抱歉，但我不同意取消团建活动的想法。团建对提高团队凝聚力很重要，取消它可能会影响团队协作。）
I see your point about simplifying the approval process by reducing the number of approval links, but I have a different view. Too few approval links may lead to inadequate review and increase the risk of errors.
（我明白你为了简化审批流程而减少审批环节的观点，但我有不同看法。审批环节过少可能导致审核不充分，增加出错风险。）
I’m not convinced by this proposal to lower the product price to compete with competitors. Lowering the price will reduce profit margins, and it’s not a sustainable competitive strategy.
（我对通过降低产品价格来与竞争对手竞争的这个提议不太信服。降价会降低利润率，且这不是可持续的竞争策略。）
I’m afraid I can’t support this decision to merge the marketing and sales departments. The two departments have different work focuses and merging them may cause confusion in job responsibilities.
（恐怕我不能支持合并市场部和销售部的这个决定。这两个部门工作重点不同，合并可能导致岗位职责混乱。）
That might work in theory—using online advertising to replace offline promotions—but in practice, offline promotions are more effective for reaching our target customers in the local market.
（从理论上讲，用线上广告替代线下推广是可行的，但在实践中，线下推广对于触达本地市场的目标客户更有效。）
I have some reservations about the plan to extend the working hours to complete the project. Extending working hours for a long time will affect employees’ work-life balance and may lead to a decline in work efficiency.
（我对通过延长工作时间来完成项目的计划有些保留意见。长期延长工作时间会影响员工的工作与生活平衡，可能导致工作效率下降。）
I don’t think this is the best approach to solve the employee performance decline—only conducting more performance evaluations. We should also provide targeted training to help employees improve their skills.
（我认为仅通过增加绩效考核次数来解决员工业绩下滑问题不是最好的方法。我们还应该提供针对性培训，帮助员工提升技能。）
While I understand your perspective that we should focus on developing the domestic market and ignore the international market, I still disagree. The international market has great potential, and giving up on it will miss development opportunities.
（虽然我理解你专注发展国内市场、忽视国际市场的观点，但我还是不同意。国际市场潜力巨大，放弃它会错失发展机会。）
I’m not sure if this method of storing all data in the cloud will achieve our data security goals. Cloud storage faces cyber threats, and we need to formulate additional security measures.
（我不确定将所有数据存储在云端的这种方法能否实现我们的数据安全目标。云存储面临网络威胁，我们需要制定额外的安全措施。）
This solution to improve the company’s brand image—only participating in industry exhibitions—seems to have some flaws. We also need to strengthen brand promotion through social media and public relations activities.
（仅通过参加行业展会来提升公司品牌形象的这个解决方案似乎存在一些缺陷。我们还需要通过社交媒体和公关活动加强品牌推广。）
I’m sorry, but I have to disagree with the idea of reducing the number of customer service staff to cut costs. Fewer customer service staff will lead to longer response times and lower customer satisfaction.
（抱歉，但我不同意为了削减成本而减少客服人员的想法。客服人员减少会导致响应时间变长，客户满意度下降。）
I see your point about using the old production equipment to avoid purchasing new equipment costs, but I have a different view. The old equipment has high energy consumption and frequent failures, which will increase production costs in the long run.
（我明白你为了避免购买新设备的成本而使用旧生产设备的观点，但我有不同看法。旧设备能耗高、故障频繁，长期来看会增加生产成本。）
I’m not convinced by this proposal to change the employee performance evaluation system to a purely quantitative one. Some work, such as creative work, is difficult to quantify, and a purely quantitative evaluation will be unfair.
（我对将员工绩效考核制度改为纯量化考核的这个提议不太信服。有些工作，比如创意类工作，难以量化，纯量化考核会不公平。）
I’m afraid I can’t support this decision to postpone the new product R&D project. Postponing it will make us fall behind competitors in the market and lose market share.
（恐怕我不能支持推迟新产品研发项目的这个决定。推迟会使我们在市场上落后于竞争对手，失去市场份额。）
That might work in theory—cooperating with a large number of small suppliers to ensure supply stability—but in practice, managing a large number of small suppliers will increase management costs and complexity.
（从理论上讲，与大量小型供应商合作以确保供应稳定是可行的，但在实践中，管理大量小型供应商会增加管理成本和复杂性。）
I have some reservations about the plan to organize the company’s annual meeting at a high-end hotel. The cost of a high-end hotel is too high, and we can choose a more cost-effective venue.
（我对在高端酒店举办公司年会的计划有些保留意见。高端酒店成本过高，我们可以选择更具成本效益的场地。）
I don’t think this is the best approach to solve the communication problems between departments—only holding more meetings. We should also establish a daily communication mechanism, such as a work group chat.
（我认为仅通过增加会议次数来解决部门间沟通问题不是最好的方法。我们还应该建立日常沟通机制，比如工作群聊。）
While I understand your perspective that we should increase the product inventory to avoid stockouts, I still disagree. Excessive inventory will occupy a lot of working capital and increase the risk of product obsolescence.
（虽然我理解你为了避免缺货而增加产品库存的观点，但我还是不同意。过多库存会占用大量流动资金，增加产品过时的风险。）
I’m not sure if this method of training new employees only through online courses will achieve our training goals. Online courses lack interaction, and new employees may not fully understand the knowledge.
（我不确定仅通过线上课程培训新员工的这种方法能否实现我们的培训目标。线上课程缺乏互动，新员工可能无法充分理解知识。）
This solution to solve the low employee participation in training—only offering training certificates—seems to have some flaws. We should also link training results to performance evaluations to increase employee motivation.
（仅通过发放培训证书来解决员工培训参与度低的问题的这个解决方案似乎存在一些缺陷。我们还应该将培训结果与绩效考核挂钩，提高员工积极性。）
I’m sorry, but I have to disagree with the idea of changing the company’s core business to enter a new industry. We lack experience in the new industry, and the risk of failure is too high.
（抱歉，但我不同意为了进入新行业而改变公司核心业务的想法。我们在新行业缺乏经验，失败风险过高。）
I see your point about increasing the number of sales promotions to stimulate consumption, but I have a different view. Frequent promotions will make customers wait for promotions to purchase, affecting regular sales.
（我明白你为了刺激消费而增加促销活动次数的观点，但我有不同看法。频繁促销会让客户等待促销再购买，影响常规销售。）
I’m not convinced by this proposal to outsource the company’s financial work. Financial work involves sensitive company information, and outsourcing may pose a risk of information leakage.
（我对将公司财务工作外包的这个提议不太信服。财务工作涉及公司敏感信息，外包可能存在信息泄露风险。）
I’m afraid I can’t support this decision to reduce the after-sales service scope to cut costs. Reducing the after-sales service scope will damage the company’s reputation and reduce customer loyalty.
（恐怕我不能支持为了削减成本而缩小售后服务范围的这个决定。缩小售后服务范围会损害公司声誉，降低客户忠诚度。）
That might work in theory—setting a high sales target to motivate the sales team—but in practice, an excessively high target will put too much pressure on employees and may lead to unethical sales behavior.
（从理论上讲，设定高销售目标来激励销售团队是可行的，但在实践中，过高的目标会给员工带来过大压力，可能导致不道德的销售行为。）
I have some reservations about the plan to upgrade the company’s office system during work hours. Upgrading during work hours will interrupt employees’ work and affect work progress.
（我对在工作时间升级公司办公系统的计划有些保留意见。工作时间升级会中断员工工作，影响工作进度。）
I don’t think this is the best approach to improve the team’s work efficiency—only urging employees to work faster. We should first optimize the work process to eliminate unnecessary links.
（我认为仅通过催促员工加快工作速度来提高团队工作效率不是最好的方法。我们应该先优化工作流程，消除不必要的环节。）
While I understand your perspective that we should focus on product sales and ignore after-sales service, I still disagree. Good after-sales service is an important factor in retaining customers and promoting repeat purchases.
（虽然我理解你专注产品销售、忽视售后服务的观点，但我还是不同意。良好的售后服务是留住客户、促进复购的重要因素。）
I’m not sure if this method of using a single supplier for raw materials to get a lower price will achieve our cost-saving goals. Relying on a single supplier will increase the risk of supply disruption.
（我不确定为了获得更低价格而仅使用一家供应商采购原材料的这种方法能否实现我们的成本节约目标。依赖单一供应商会增加供应中断的风险。）
This solution to solve the company’s brand awareness problem—only launching a large-scale advertising campaign—seems to have some flaws. We also need to improve product quality and customer service to enhance brand reputation.
（仅通过开展大规模广告宣传来解决公司品牌知名度低的问题的这个解决方案似乎存在一些缺陷。我们还需要通过提高产品质量和客户服务来提升品牌口碑。）
协助需求提出
Could you assist me with [task]?
（你能在 [任务] 上帮我一下吗？）
I need some input from you on [topic].
（关于 [主题]，我需要听听你的意见。）
Can someone clarify this point for me?
（有人能帮我解释一下这一点吗？）
I’m not familiar with this. Can you explain it?
（我对此不熟悉，你能解释一下吗？）
I’m having trouble understanding [concept]. Could you elaborate?
（我不太理解 [概念]，你能详细说明一下吗？）
I need your expertise on [area].
（我需要你在 [领域] 的专业知识。）
Would you mind sharing your experience with [process]?
（你介意分享一下你在 [流程] 方面的经验吗？）
I’m stuck on this issue. Any suggestions?
（我在这个问题上卡住了，有什么建议吗？）
Can you guide me on how to [action]?
（你能指导我如何 [行动] 吗？）
I’m looking for some advice on [problem].
（我想就 [问题] 寻求一些建议。）
Could you assist me with sorting out the project progress data? I’m swamped with other tasks and don’t have enough time to handle it.
（你能帮我整理一下项目进度数据吗？我手头其他任务太多，没时间处理这个了。）
I need some input from you on the new marketing plan. You have rich experience in this field, and your opinions will be very helpful for optimizing the plan.
（关于新的营销计划，我需要听听你的意见。你在这个领域经验丰富，你的看法对优化计划很有帮助。）
Can someone clarify this point in the contract for me? The terms related to liability division are too professional, and I can’t fully understand them.
（有人能帮我解释一下合同中的这一点吗？关于责任划分的条款太专业了，我理解不透彻。）
I’m not familiar with the new office software. Can you explain how to use the data analysis function in it? I need to use it to complete the report.
（我对新的办公软件不熟悉，你能解释一下怎么使用里面的数据分析功能吗？我需要用它来完成报告。）
I’m having trouble understanding the customer’s needs for product customization. Could you elaborate on what they mean by “special functional requirements”?
（我不太理解客户对产品定制的需求，你能详细说明一下他们说的 “特殊功能要求” 是什么意思吗？）
I need your expertise on supplier management. We’re having frequent disputes with a supplier recently, and I don’t know how to resolve them effectively.
（我需要你在供应商管理方面的专业知识。最近我们和一家供应商频繁发生纠纷，我不知道如何有效解决。）
Would you mind sharing your experience with handling customer complaints? I’ve just taken over the customer service work and don’t know how to deal with angry customers properly.
（你介意分享一下你处理客户投诉的经验吗？我刚接手客服工作，不知道如何妥善应对愤怒的客户。）
I’m stuck on this technical problem in the project— the system keeps crashing during the test. Any suggestions on how to find the cause and fix it?
（我在项目中的这个技术问题上卡住了 —— 测试时系统总是崩溃。关于如何找到原因并解决，有什么建议吗？）
Can you guide me on how to apply for the budget adjustment? I’ve never done this before, and I don’t know the specific process and required materials.
（你能指导我如何申请预算调整吗？我以前从没做过，不知道具体流程和所需材料。）
I’m looking for some advice on improving the team’s work efficiency. The team’s progress has been slow recently, and I don’t know what measures to take.
（我想就提高团队工作效率寻求一些建议。最近团队进度很慢，我不知道该采取什么措施。）
Could you assist me with preparing the meeting materials for tomorrow’s client meeting? I need to collect relevant product information and case studies, but I’m short on time.
（你能帮我准备明天客户会议的会议资料吗？我需要收集相关产品信息和案例，但时间不够了。）
I need some input from you on the employee training content. You know the team’s skill gaps well, so your suggestions on what content to include will be very valuable.
（关于员工培训内容，我需要听听你的意见。你很了解团队的技能差距，所以你对培训内容的建议会很有价值。）
Can someone clarify this financial report indicator for me? The “return on investment (ROI)” calculation method is confusing me, and I need to understand it to explain it to the leadership.
（有人能帮我解释一下这份财务报告中的这个指标吗？“投资回报率
（ROI）” 的计算方法让我很困惑，我需要理解它以便向领导层解释。）
I’m not familiar with the local market conditions in the southern region. Can you explain the consumer preferences and competitive landscape there? We’re planning to expand the market there.
（我对南方地区的本地市场情况不熟悉，你能解释一下那里的消费者偏好和竞争格局吗？我们计划在那里开拓市场。）
I’m having trouble understanding the new company policy on expense reimbursement. Could you elaborate on which expenses are reimbursable and the required supporting documents?
（我不太理解公司新的费用报销政策，你能详细说明一下哪些费用可以报销以及所需的证明材料吗？）
I need your expertise on project risk assessment. We’re about to start a new project, and I don’t know how to identify potential risks comprehensively.
（我需要你在项目风险评估方面的专业知识。我们即将启动一个新项目，我不知道如何全面识别潜在风险。）
Would you mind sharing your experience with organizing team-building activities? I need to plan one for the team next month, but I don’t know how to choose a suitable form and location.
（你介意分享一下你组织团建活动的经验吗？我下个月需要为团队策划一次团建，但不知道如何选择合适的形式和地点。）
I’m stuck on this problem of low employee participation in the new system training. Despite repeated reminders, few employees sign up. Any suggestions on how to increase participation?
（我在新系统培训员工参与度低的这个问题上卡住了。尽管反复提醒，报名的员工还是很少。关于如何提高参与度，有什么建议吗？）
Can you guide me on how to negotiate with suppliers on price? This is my first time leading a negotiation, and I’m worried about not getting a good price.
（你能指导我如何与供应商进行价格谈判吗？这是我第一次主导谈判，担心谈不到好价格。）
I’m looking for some advice on how to handle the conflict between two team members. Their disagreement on work methods has affected the team’s work progress.
（我想就如何处理两名团队成员之间的冲突寻求一些建议。他们在工作方法上的分歧影响了团队的工作进度。）
Could you assist me with proofreading the English version of the product manual? My English is not very good, and I’m worried about making grammatical errors or inaccurate expressions.
（你能帮我校对一下产品手册的英文版吗？我的英语不太好，担心出现语法错误或表达不准确的问题。）
I need some input from you on the new product design. You have a good understanding of customer needs, so your opinions on the product’s appearance and functions will be very helpful.
（关于新产品设计，我需要听听你的意见。你很了解客户需求，所以你对产品外观和功能的建议会很有帮助。）
Can someone clarify this point in the project plan for me? The “critical path” mentioned in it is unclear to me, and I need to know which tasks are the most important.
（有人能帮我解释一下项目计划中的这一点吗？里面提到的 “关键路径” 我不太明白，需要知道哪些任务是最重要的。）
I’m not familiar with the process of applying for patents. Can you explain the steps, the time required, and the relevant agencies involved? We have a new invention that needs to be patented.
（我对申请专利的流程不熟悉，你能解释一下步骤、所需时间以及涉及的相关机构吗？我们有一项新发明需要申请专利。）
I’m having trouble understanding the data analysis report on market trends. Could you elaborate on what the “market growth rate” and “market share forecast” in the report represent?
（我不太理解这份市场趋势数据分析报告，你能详细说明一下报告中的 “市场增长率” 和 “市场份额预测” 代表什么吗？）
I need your expertise on corporate culture promotion. We want to promote the company’s new corporate culture, but I don’t know which channels and methods are more effective.
（我需要你在企业文化推广方面的专业知识。我们想推广公司新的企业文化，但不知道哪些渠道和方法更有效。）
Would you mind sharing your experience with writing work reports? I always struggle to organize the content clearly and highlight the key points, and I need to improve this skill.
（你介意分享一下你写工作报告的经验吗？我总是难以把内容组织清楚并突出重点，需要提升这项技能。）
I’m stuck on this problem of the supplier’s failure to deliver raw materials on time. We’ve communicated with them many times, but there’s no improvement. Any suggestions on how to urge them or find alternative solutions?
（我在供应商未能按时交付原材料的这个问题上卡住了。我们已经和他们沟通了很多次，但没有改善。关于如何催促他们或寻找替代解决方案，有什么建议吗？）
Can you guide me on how to use the project management tool to assign tasks and track progress? I’ve just started using it, and I’m not familiar with its functions.
（你能指导我如何使用项目管理工具分配任务和跟踪进度吗？我刚开始使用，对它的功能不熟悉。）
I’m looking for some advice on how to improve my public speaking skills. I need to give a presentation at the upcoming company meeting, and I’m nervous about it.
（我想就如何提高公开演讲技能寻求一些建议。我需要在即将到来的公司会议上做汇报，对此感到很紧张。）
Could you assist me with collecting information about competitors’ new products? I need to know their product features, prices, and marketing strategies to help us adjust our own product plan.
（你能帮我收集竞争对手新产品的信息吗？我需要了解他们的产品特点、价格和营销策略，以便我们调整自己的产品计划。）
I need some input from you on the office layout optimization plan. You know the team’s work habits well, so your suggestions on how to arrange workstations and meeting rooms will be very practical.
（关于办公布局优化计划，我需要听听你的意见。你很了解团队的工作习惯，所以你对工作站和会议室布置的建议会很实用。）
Can someone clarify this point in the labor law for me? The regulations on “overtime pay calculation” are confusing, and I need to understand them to ensure the company’s salary payment is compliant.
（有人能帮我解释一下劳动法中的这一点吗？关于 “加班费计算” 的规定让我很困惑，我需要理解它以确保公司薪资发放合规。）
I’m not familiar with the process of organizing industry exhibitions. Can you explain how to book a booth, prepare exhibition materials, and attract visitors to our booth?
（我对组织行业展会的流程不熟悉，你能解释一下如何预订展位、准备展会资料以及吸引观众到我们展位吗？）
I’m having trouble understanding the customer’s feedback on the product. They mentioned “poor user experience,” but didn’t specify the details. Could you elaborate on possible aspects they might be referring to?
（我不太理解客户对产品的反馈，他们提到 “用户体验差”，但没有具体说明细节。你能详细说明一下他们可能指的是哪些方面吗？）
I need your expertise on data security protection. We’ve recently encountered some data leakage risks, and I don’t know how to formulate effective protection measures.
（我需要你在数据安全保护方面的专业知识。我们最近遇到了一些数据泄露风险，不知道如何制定有效的保护措施。）
Would you mind sharing your experience with handling emergency situations in projects? For example, if a key team member suddenly resigns, how do you ensure the project proceeds smoothly?
（你介意分享一下你处理项目中紧急情况的经验吗？比如，如果核心团队成员突然离职，如何确保项目顺利推进？）
I’m stuck on this problem of the new product failing to pass the quality inspection. We’ve checked the production process many times, but still can’t find the reason. Any suggestions on how to conduct a more in-depth investigation?
（我在新产品未能通过质量检测的这个问题上卡住了。我们已经检查了很多次生产流程，但还是找不到原因。关于如何进行更深入的调查，有什么建议吗？）
Can you guide me on how to draft a cooperation agreement with a partner company? I need to ensure that the rights and obligations of both parties are clearly defined to avoid future disputes.
（你能指导我如何起草与合作公司的合作协议吗？我需要确保明确双方的权利和义务，避免未来产生纠纷。）
I’m looking for some advice on how to balance work and life. Recently, I’ve been working overtime frequently, and my physical and mental state is not good. I want to find a way to improve this situation.
（我想就如何平衡工作与生活寻求一些建议。最近我频繁加班，身心状态都不好，想找到改善这种情况的方法。）
问题提问环节
Are there any questions so far?
（到目前为止有什么问题吗？）
Does anyone have any questions regarding [topic]?
（关于 [主题]，有人有问题吗？）
If you have any questions, feel free to ask now.
（如果有任何问题，现在可以提问。）
Let’s open the floor for questions.
（我们开始提问环节。）
Do you have any questions about the plan?
（你对这个计划有什么问题吗？）
I’ll be happy to answer any questions you may have.
（我很乐意回答你们的任何问题。）
Are there any clarifications needed?
（需要进一步说明吗？）
Any questions or concerns?
（有任何问题或担忧吗？）
If you’re unclear about anything, please ask.
（如果有任何不明白的地方，请提问。）
Let’s take a few minutes for questions.
（我们花几分钟时间来提问。）
Are there any questions so far about the project progress report I just presented? I can explain any unclear data or progress details again.
（关于我刚才展示的项目进度报告，到目前为止有什么问题吗？任何不清楚的数据或进度细节，我都可以再解释一遍。）
Does anyone have any questions regarding the new marketing strategy for the second half of the year? For example, the choice of promotional channels or the setting of sales targets.
（关于下半年新的营销策略，有人有问题吗？比如推广渠道的选择或销售目标的设定。）
If you have any questions about the employee training plan, feel free to ask now. Whether it’s about the training content, time arrangement, or trainers, I’ll answer them in detail.
（如果对员工培训计划有任何问题，现在可以随时提问。无论是培训内容、时间安排还是培训师，我都会详细解答。）
Let’s open the floor for questions about the cooperation plan with [Partner Company]. You can ask about the cooperation terms, profit distribution, or implementation steps.
（我们开始关于与 [合作公司] 合作方案的提问环节。大家可以询问合作条款、利润分配或实施步骤等问题。）
Do you have any questions about the budget allocation plan for next quarter? Especially regarding the fund distribution among different departments or the reasons for adjusting the budget of certain projects.
（你对下季度的预算分配计划有什么问题吗？尤其是不同部门之间的资金分配，或者某些项目预算调整的原因。）
I’ll be happy to answer any questions you may have about the new office software. Whether it’s about its basic functions, advanced operations, or troubleshooting methods, I’m here to help.
（我很乐意回答你们关于新办公软件的任何问题。无论是基本功能、高级操作还是故障排除方法，我都能提供帮助。）
Are there any clarifications needed about the new company policy on business trips? For example, the reimbursement standards for transportation and accommodation or the application process for business trips.
（关于公司新的差旅政策，需要进一步说明吗？比如交通和住宿的报销标准，或者差旅申请流程。）
Any questions or concerns about the product quality improvement measures we discussed? You can express your worries about the implementation effect or put forward supplementary suggestions.
（关于我们讨论的产品质量改进措施，有任何问题或担忧吗？大家可以表达对实施效果的担忧，或者提出补充建议。）
If you’re unclear about anything in the customer feedback analysis report, please ask. For example, how we collected the feedback or how we categorized the customer complaints.
（如果对客户反馈分析报告中的任何内容不明白，请提问。比如我们是如何收集反馈的，或者如何对客户投诉进行分类的。）
Let’s take a few minutes for questions about the team’s work arrangement next month. You can ask about your own work tasks, the collaboration requirements with other departments, or the time limits for tasks.
（我们花几分钟时间来提问关于下个月团队工作安排的问题。大家可以询问自己的工作任务、与其他部门的协作要求，或者任务的时间期限。）
Are there any questions so far about the technical solution for the new project? I can explain the technical principles, the selection of equipment, or the risk prevention measures in detail.
（关于新项目的技术方案，到目前为止有什么问题吗？我可以详细解释技术原理、设备选择或风险防范措施。）
Does anyone have any questions regarding the supplier selection criteria we proposed? For example, the evaluation indicators for supplier strength or the inspection methods for product quality.
（关于我们提出的供应商选择标准，有人有问题吗？比如对供应商实力的评价指标，或者对产品质量的检验方法。）
If you have any questions about the company’s annual meeting plan, feel free to ask now. Whether it’s about the meeting venue, agenda, or the arrangement of evening activities, I’ll answer them.
（如果对公司年会计划有任何问题，现在可以随时提问。无论是会议场地、议程，还是晚间活动安排，我都会解答。）
Let’s open the floor for questions about the market research results. You can ask about the sample size of the research, the data analysis methods, or the reliability of the research conclusions.
（我们开始关于市场调研结果的提问环节。大家可以询问调研的样本量、数据分析方法，或者调研结论的可靠性。）
Do you have any questions about the plan to optimize the customer service process? For example, the adjustment of the service response time or the setting of the customer complaint handling process.
（你对客户服务流程优化计划有什么问题吗？比如服务响应时间的调整，或者客户投诉处理流程的设定。）
I’ll be happy to answer any questions you may have about the employee performance evaluation system revision. Whether it’s about the adjustment of evaluation indicators or the connection between evaluation results and incentives, I’ll explain clearly.
（我很乐意回答你们关于员工绩效考核制度修订的任何问题。无论是考核指标的调整，还是考核结果与激励的挂钩方式，我都会解释清楚。）
Are there any clarifications needed about the new product launch plan? For example, the launch time in different regions or the pricing strategy for the new product.
（关于新产品推出计划，需要进一步说明吗？比如在不同地区的推出时间，或者新产品的定价策略。）
Any questions or concerns about the office relocation plan? You can ask about the moving time, the arrangement of new workstations, or the handling of personal office supplies.
（关于办公地点搬迁计划，有任何问题或担忧吗？大家可以询问搬迁时间、新工作站的安排，或者个人办公用品的处理方式。）
If you’re unclear about anything in the financial report for the previous quarter, please ask. For example, the calculation method of profits or the reasons for the change in operating costs.
（如果对上个季度的财务报告中的任何内容不明白，请提问。比如利润的计算方式，或者运营成本变化的原因。）
Let’s take a few minutes for questions about the risk assessment report of the project. You can ask about the identification of potential risks or the formulation of response strategies for key risks.
（我们花几分钟时间来提问关于项目风险评估报告的问题。大家可以询问潜在风险的识别，或者关键风险的应对策略制定。）
Are there any questions so far about the plan to promote the company’s brand image? I can explain the selection of promotion channels, the content of promotional activities, or the expected effect of promotion.
（关于提升公司品牌形象的计划，到目前为止有什么问题吗？我可以解释推广渠道的选择、推广活动的内容，或者推广的预期效果。）
Does anyone have any questions regarding the training plan for new employees? For example, the duration of the training, the assessment methods after training, or the arrangement of mentors.
（关于新员工培训计划，有人有问题吗？比如培训时长、培训后的考核方式，或者导师的安排。）
If you have any questions about the cooperation agreement with the customer, feel free to ask now. Whether it’s about the delivery terms, payment methods, or after-sales service commitments, I’ll answer them in detail.
（如果对与客户的合作协议有任何问题，现在可以随时提问。无论是交货条款、付款方式，还是售后服务承诺，我都会详细解答。）
Let’s open the floor for questions about the work progress of the R&D team. You can ask about the progress of key R&D tasks, the difficulties encountered, or the expected completion time of the project.
（我们开始关于研发团队工作进展的提问环节。大家可以询问关键研发任务的进展、遇到的困难，或者项目的预期完成时间。）
Do you have any questions about the plan to reduce production costs? For example, the specific measures to optimize the production process or the expected cost-saving amount.
（你对降低生产成本的计划有什么问题吗？比如优化生产流程的具体措施，或者预期的成本节约金额。）
I’ll be happy to answer any questions you may have about the data security protection plan. Whether it’s about the encryption method of data storage or the access control of sensitive information, I’ll explain clearly.
（我很乐意回答你们关于数据安全保护计划的任何问题。无论是数据存储的加密方式，还是敏感信息的访问控制，我都会解释清楚。）
Are there any clarifications needed about the company’s development strategy for the next three years? For example, the focus of market expansion or the direction of product innovation.
（关于公司未来三年的发展战略，需要进一步说明吗？比如市场拓展的重点，或者产品创新的方向。）
Any questions or concerns about the team-building activity plan? You can ask about the activity content, the safety precautions, or the cost of the activity.
（关于团建活动计划，有任何问题或担忧吗？大家可以询问活动内容、安全注意事项，或者活动费用。）

（如果对供应商绩效评估报告中的任何内容不明白，请提问。比如对供应商交货及时性的评价标准，或者对不合格供应商的处理方式。）

（我们花几分钟时间来提问关于改善办公环境的计划的问题。大家可以询问办公区域的装修、办公设备的更换，或者工作氛围的改善措施。）
