会后跟进落实
I’ll follow up with you regarding [task] by [date].
（我会在 [日期] 前就 [任务] 与你跟进。）
Please make sure to complete [task] by the deadline.
（请确保在截止日期前完成 [任务]。）
Let’s set up a follow-up meeting to review the progress.
（我们安排一次跟进会议来检查进展情况。）
I’ll send you a reminder about [action] next week.
（下周我会就 [行动] 给你发提醒。）
If there are any issues, let’s address them immediately.
（如果有任何问题，我们立即处理。）
Keep me updated on the status of [project].
（让我了解 [项目] 的进展情况。）
We need to monitor the implementation of [plan] closely.
（我们需要密切监控 [计划] 的执行情况。）
Please provide a progress report by [date].
（请在 [日期] 前提供进度报告。）
Let’s stay in touch and ensure everything goes smoothly.
（我们保持联系，确保一切顺利。）
I’ll follow up with the relevant parties to get this done.
（我会与相关方跟进，确保事情完成。）
I’ll follow up with you regarding the customer feedback collection task by the end of this week. I need to know how many customers you’ve contacted and what feedback you’ve received.
（我会在本周末前就客户反馈收集任务与你跟进。我需要知道你已经联系了多少客户，以及收到了哪些反馈。）
Please make sure to complete the draft of the marketing plan by next Friday. The management will review it at the meeting on the following Monday, so there’s no room for delay.
（请确保在下周五前完成营销计划草案。管理层将在下周一下的会议上审核，所以不能有任何延误。）
Let’s set up a follow-up meeting next Wednesday at 3:00 PM to review the progress of the project. Please prepare a detailed progress report and bring it to the meeting.
（我们安排下周三下午 3 点召开一次跟进会议，检查项目进展。请准备一份详细的进度报告，并带到会议上。）
I’ll send you a reminder about submitting the expense reimbursement application next week. Please sort out the relevant invoices and supporting documents in advance to avoid missing the deadline.
（下周我会就提交费用报销申请给你发提醒。请提前整理好相关发票和证明材料，避免错过截止日期。）
If there are any issues with the supplier’s raw material delivery, let’s address them immediately. Delays in raw materials will affect the production schedule, so we can’t wait.
（如果供应商的原材料交货有任何问题，我们立即处理。原材料延误将影响生产计划，不能拖延。）
Keep me updated on the status of the new product testing every two days. Whether it’s progress, problems encountered, or test results, please send me a brief report.
（每两天让我了解一下新产品测试的进展情况。无论是进度、遇到的问题还是测试结果，都请给我发一份简要报告。）
We need to monitor the implementation of the cost-saving plan closely. Every week, we should check the actual cost-saving amount and analyze whether the measures are effective.
（我们需要密切监控成本节约计划的执行情况。每周都要检查实际节约的成本金额，并分析措施是否有效。）
Please provide a progress report on the employee training by the end of this month. The report should include the number of employees trained, the training content completed, and the employees’ feedback.
（请在本月底前提供员工培训的进度报告。报告应包括培训人数、完成的培训内容以及员工的反馈情况。）
Let’s stay in touch via WeChat regarding the cooperation with [Partner Company]. If there are any changes in the cooperation terms or progress, please inform me in a timely manner to ensure everything goes smoothly.
（关于与 [合作公司] 的合作，我们通过微信保持联系。如果合作条款或进度有任何变化，请及时告知我，确保一切顺利。）
I’ll follow up with the technical team and the procurement department to get the equipment purchase done. I’ll coordinate with them to confirm the purchase list and delivery time.
（我会与技术团队和采购部门跟进，确保设备采购事宜完成。我会协调他们确认采购清单和交货时间。）
I’ll follow up with you regarding the revision of the employee handbook by next Tuesday. I need to know which chapters have been revised and whether there are any controversial clauses that need further discussion.
（我会在下周二前就员工手册修订与你跟进。我需要知道哪些章节已经修订，以及是否有有争议的条款需要进一步讨论。）
Please make sure to complete the data analysis of the market research results by this Friday. The analysis results will be used in the next marketing strategy meeting, so accuracy is crucial.
（请确保在本周五前完成市场调研结果的数据分析。分析结果将用于下一次营销策略会议，所以准确性至关重要。）
Let’s set up a follow-up meeting next Thursday to review the progress of the customer complaint handling. Please collect the handling status of each complaint and prepare a summary report.
（我们安排下周四召开一次跟进会议，检查客户投诉处理的进展。请收集每一项投诉的处理情况，并准备一份总结报告。）
I’ll send you a reminder about the project milestone check next week. The milestone is on the 15th of this month, and we need to confirm whether all tasks under the milestone have been completed.
（下周我会就项目里程碑检查给你发提醒。本月 15 日是里程碑节点，我们需要确认该节点下的所有任务是否都已完成。）
If there are any issues with the office software installation, let’s address them immediately. Many employees need to use the software to work, and prolonged problems will affect work efficiency.
（如果办公软件安装有任何问题，我们立即处理。很多员工需要使用该软件工作，问题拖延会影响工作效率。）
Keep me updated on the status of the supplier performance evaluation. After evaluating each supplier, please send me the evaluation results and your suggestions on whether to continue cooperating.
（让我了解供应商绩效评估的进展情况。评估完每个供应商后，请将评估结果以及你关于是否继续合作的建议发给我。）
We need to monitor the implementation of the new product launch plan closely. Every day, we should track the sales volume, customer feedback, and inventory status to adjust the plan in a timely manner if there are any deviations.
（我们需要密切监控新产品推出计划的执行情况。每天都要跟踪销量、客户反馈和库存状况，若有偏差及时调整计划。）
Please provide a progress report on the office relocation preparation by next Monday. The report should cover the progress of venue decoration, equipment purchase, and staff workstation arrangement.
（请在下周一前提供办公搬迁准备工作的进度报告。报告需涵盖场地装修、设备采购以及员工工位安排的进展。）
Let’s stay in touch through weekly emails to ensure the smooth progress of the cross-departmental project. In each email, please briefly describe the work completed, the work to be done next, and any problems encountered.
（我们通过每周邮件保持联系，确保跨部门项目顺利推进。每次邮件中，请简要说明已完成的工作、接下来要做的工作以及遇到的问题。）
I’ll follow up with the finance department to get the budget approval done. I’ll explain the necessity of the project budget to them and urge them to complete the approval process as soon as possible.
（我会与财务部门跟进，确保预算审批事宜完成。我会向他们说明项目预算的必要性，并催促尽快完成审批流程。）
I’ll follow up with you regarding the preparation of the annual meeting materials by the 10th of next month. I need to confirm whether the meeting agenda, guest list, and promotional materials are all ready.
（我会在下个月 10 日前就年会材料准备工作与你跟进。我需要确认会议议程、嘉宾名单以及宣传物料是否都已准备就绪。）
Please make sure to complete the translation of the product manual by the end of this month. The translated version will be used for overseas market promotion, so it must be accurate and in line with local language habits.
（请确保在本月底前完成产品手册的翻译工作。翻译版本将用于海外市场推广，因此必须准确且符合当地语言习惯。）
Let’s set up a follow-up meeting next Friday morning to review the progress of the employee performance evaluation. Please collect the evaluation forms from each department and sort out the key problems found in the evaluation.
（我们安排下周五上午召开跟进会议，检查员工绩效考核的进展。请收集各部门的考核表，并整理考核中发现的关键问题。）
I’ll send you a reminder about the submission of the project progress report next week. The report needs to include not only the progress data but also an analysis of the factors affecting the progress.
（下周我会就项目进度报告的提交给你发提醒。报告不仅要包含进度数据，还要有影响进度因素的分析。）
If there are any issues with the customer’s order production, let’s address them immediately. We have promised the customer a delivery time, and any delays will damage the company’s reputation.
（如果客户订单生产有任何问题，我们立即处理。我们已向客户承诺了交货时间，任何延误都会损害公司声誉。）
Keep me updated on the status of the industry exhibition preparation every three days. Whether it’s the design of the booth, the preparation of exhibition products, or the arrangement of on-site staff, please let me know the latest progress.
（每三天让我了解一次行业展会准备工作的进展。无论是展位设计、参展产品准备，还是现场人员安排，都请告知我最新情况。）
We need to monitor the implementation of the data security protection plan closely. Regularly check whether the data encryption measures are effective and whether employees are complying with the data access regulations.
（我们需要密切监控数据安全保护计划的执行情况。定期检查数据加密措施是否有效，员工是否遵守数据访问规定。）
Please provide a progress report on the supplier cooperation negotiation by next Wednesday. The report should specify the progress of the negotiation on price, delivery terms, and after-sales service.
（请在下周三前提供供应商合作谈判的进度报告。报告需明确在价格、交货条款以及售后服务方面的谈判进展。）
Let’s stay in touch via phone calls if there are urgent matters related to the new project. Timely communication can help us solve problems quickly and avoid affecting the project schedule.
（若有与新项目相关的紧急事宜，我们通过电话保持联系。及时沟通能帮助我们快速解决问题，避免影响项目进度。）
I’ll follow up with the marketing team to get the promotional campaign launched. I’ll confirm the launch time of each promotional channel with them and ensure that the promotional materials are in place.
（我会与营销团队跟进，确保促销活动顺利开展。我会和他们确认各推广渠道的上线时间，确保宣传物料到位。）
I’ll follow up with you regarding the sorting of the meeting minutes by the end of today. Please ensure that the minutes clearly record the decisions made, action items, and responsible persons.
（我会在今天结束前就会议纪要的整理与你跟进。请确保纪要清晰记录做出的决策、行动事项以及负责人。）
Please make sure to complete the inspection of the production equipment by this weekend. If any equipment is found to be faulty, please report it immediately and arrange for repairs.
（请确保在本周末前完成生产设备的检查工作。若发现任何设备故障，请立即上报并安排维修。）
Let’s set up a follow-up meeting next Tuesday afternoon to review the progress of the customer satisfaction improvement measures. Please collect the customer satisfaction data after the implementation of the measures and analyze the improvement effect.
（我们安排下周二下午召开跟进会议，检查客户满意度提升措施的进展。请收集措施实施后的客户满意度数据，并分析提升效果。）
I’ll send you a reminder about the training of new employees next week. The training will start on the 20th of this month, and you need to confirm the list of trainees and the training materials in advance.
（下周我会就新员工培训给你发提醒。培训将于本月 20 日开始，你需提前确认参训人员名单和培训资料。）
If there are any issues with the cooperation agreement signing with [Partner Company], let’s address them immediately. The sooner the agreement is signed, the sooner we can start the formal cooperation.
（如果与 [合作公司] 的合作协议签订有任何问题，我们立即处理。协议签订得越早，我们就能越早开始正式合作。）
Keep me updated on the status of the budget adjustment application. If there is any feedback from the leadership, please inform me in a timely manner to make corresponding adjustments.
（让我了解预算调整申请的进展情况。若领导层有任何反馈，请及时告知我，以便做出相应调整。）
We need to monitor the implementation of the team-building activity plan closely. Confirm the activity venue, transportation arrangement, and safety measures one week in advance to ensure the activity goes smoothly.
（我们需要密切监控团建活动计划的执行情况。提前一周确认活动场地、交通安排以及安全措施，确保活动顺利进行。）
Please provide a progress report on the new product patent application by the 15th of next month. The report should include the progress of the application materials preparation and the communication status with the patent office.
（请在下个月 15 日前提供新产品专利申请的进度报告。报告需包含申请材料准备的进展以及与专利局的沟通情况。）
Let’s stay in touch and share the latest information about the market competition. If competitors launch new products or promotional activities, we need to respond in a timely manner.
（我们保持联系，分享市场竞争的最新信息。若竞争对手推出新产品或促销活动，我们需及时做出应对。）
I’ll follow up with the relevant departments to get the office software upgrade done. I’ll coordinate the upgrade time to avoid affecting the daily work of employees and ensure that the upgrade is completed successfully.
（我会与相关部门跟进，确保办公软件升级工作完成。我会协调升级时间，避免影响员工日常工作，确保升级顺利完成。）
技术故障处理
I’m having trouble connecting to the meeting.
（我连接会议时遇到问题。）
Can you hear me? My audio seems to be working.
（你能听到我说话吗？我的音频好像没问题。）
I’m experiencing some technical glitches.
（我遇到了一些技术故障。）
Could you share your screen again? I lost it.
（你能再分享一下屏幕吗？我看不到了。）
My video isn’t showing. I’ll try to fix it.
（我的视频显示不出来，我试试修复。）
Is anyone else having issues with the internet connection?
（还有人网络连接有问题吗？）
The call keeps dropping. Let’s switch to another platform.
（通话一直中断，我们换个平台吧。）
I can’t access the shared document.
（我无法访问共享文档。）
Could you mute your microphone if you’re not speaking?
（如果你不发言，请把麦克风静音好吗？）
There’s a lot of background noise. Can you find a quieter place?
（背景噪音很大，你能找个安静点的地方吗？）
I’m having trouble connecting to the video meeting. Every time I enter the link, the page shows a “connection timeout” prompt.
（我连接视频会议时遇到问题。每次输入链接，页面都显示 “连接超时” 提示。）
Can you hear me clearly? My audio seems to be working, but I’m not sure if the volume is too low for everyone.
（你们能清楚听到我说话吗？我的音频好像没问题，但不确定音量对大家来说是否太低。）
I’m experiencing some technical glitches with the project management software. When I try to assign tasks, the system freezes and doesn’t respond.
（我在使用项目管理软件时遇到一些技术故障。尝试分配任务时，系统会卡住，没有响应。）
Could you share your screen again? I lost it when I switched between browser tabs. I need to see the data on the slide you were showing earlier.
（你能再分享一下屏幕吗？我切换浏览器标签页时看不到了。我需要看你之前展示的幻灯片上的数据。）
My video isn’t showing in the meeting. I’ll try to fix it by restarting the camera and checking the camera permission settings.
（会议中我的视频显示不出来。我会通过重启摄像头和检查摄像头权限设置来尝试修复。）
Is anyone else having issues with the internet connection? My network speed is very slow, and the meeting video keeps buffering.
（还有人网络连接有问题吗？我的网速很慢，会议视频一直缓冲。）
The call keeps dropping every five minutes. Let’s switch to another platform like Zoom instead of the current one to ensure the meeting continues smoothly.
（通话每五分钟就会中断一次。我们换个平台吧，比如 Zoom，而不是现在这个，以确保会议顺利进行。）
I can’t access the shared document in the team folder. It shows “permission denied” when I click on the link. Could you check my access rights?
（我无法访问团队文件夹中的共享文档。点击链接时显示 “权限不足”，你能检查一下我的访问权限吗？）
Could you mute your microphone if you’re not speaking? There’s a lot of rustling noise coming from your side, which is interfering with the meeting discussion.
（如果你不发言，请把麦克风静音好吗？你那边有很多沙沙声，干扰了会议讨论。）
There’s a lot of background noise, like the sound of a printer and people talking. Can you find a quieter place, such as a meeting room, to participate in the meeting?
（背景噪音很大，有打印机声和人声。你能找个安静点的地方，比如会议室，来参加会议吗？）
I’m having trouble connecting to the company’s internal system to retrieve project data. The login page keeps refreshing and won’t let me enter my account information.
（我连接公司内部系统调取项目数据时遇到问题。登录页面一直刷新，无法输入账号信息。）
Can you hear me now? I just adjusted the microphone settings. Earlier, I think the audio was being picked up by the wrong device.
（现在能听到我说话了吗？我刚调整了麦克风设置。之前我觉得音频可能被错误的设备接收了。）
I’m experiencing some technical glitches with the data analysis tool. When I import the Excel file, the tool crashes and closes automatically.
（我使用数据分析工具时遇到一些技术故障。导入 Excel 文件时，工具会崩溃并自动关闭。）
Could you share your screen again and zoom in on the chart? I lost the screen share earlier, and now I can’t see the details of the data in the chart clearly.
（你能再分享屏幕并把图表放大吗？我之前看不到屏幕共享了，现在看不清图表中的数据细节。）
My video isn’t showing even after restarting the computer. I’ll check if there’s a problem with the camera driver and update it if necessary.
（即使重启电脑，我的视频还是显示不出来。我会检查摄像头驱动是否有问题，必要时进行更新。）
Is anyone else having issues with the online whiteboard? I can’t write or draw on it, and all the buttons are grayed out.
（还有人在线白板上遇到问题吗？我无法在上面书写或绘图，所有按钮都是灰色的，无法点击。）
The call keeps dropping when we discuss the technical details of the project. Let’s switch to a more stable communication platform and send the meeting link to everyone again.
（我们讨论项目技术细节时，通话一直中断。我们换个更稳定的沟通平台，并重新把会议链接发给大家。）
I can’t access the shared folder containing the meeting materials. It says the folder path is invalid. Could you re-send the correct link to me via email?
（我无法访问包含会议资料的共享文件夹，提示文件夹路径无效。你能通过邮件把正确的链接重新发给我吗？）
Could you mute your microphone temporarily? There’s a continuous beeping sound from your device, which is making it hard for everyone to focus on the discussion.
（你能暂时把麦克风静音吗？你的设备有持续的蜂鸣声，让大家很难专注于讨论。）
There’s a lot of background noise from the street outside your office. Can you close the window or move to a position away from the window to reduce the noise?
（你办公室外的街道有很大背景噪音。你能关上窗户或搬到远离窗户的位置，以减少噪音吗？）
I’m having trouble connecting to the VPN, which is required to access the project documents. The VPN client keeps showing “connection failed” without a specific reason.
（我连接 VPN 时遇到问题，而访问项目文档需要 VPN。VPN 客户端一直显示 “连接失败”，没有具体原因。）
Can you hear me clearly now? I switched to a wired headset instead of the wireless one, which I think was causing the audio interference earlier.
（现在能清楚听到我说话了吗？我换成了有线耳机，而不是之前的无线耳机，我觉得之前的无线耳机导致了音频干扰。）
I’m experiencing some technical glitches with the presentation software. The slides are stuck on the first page, and I can’t navigate to the next one even after clicking the forward button.
（我使用演示软件时遇到一些技术故障。幻灯片卡在第一页，即使点击前进按钮也无法切换到下一页。）
Could you share your screen again and go back to the previous slide? I lost the screen share and missed the key points you explained on that slide.
（你能再分享屏幕并回到上一张幻灯片吗？我之前看不到屏幕共享了，错过了你在那张幻灯片上讲解的要点。）
My video isn’t showing, and the camera indicator light isn’t on either. I’ll check if the camera is physically connected properly or if there’s a hardware issue.
（我的视频显示不出来，摄像头指示灯也没亮。我会检查摄像头是否物理连接正常，或者是否存在硬件问题。）
Is anyone else having issues with the meeting recording function? I tried to start recording, but the system prompted that “recording permission is not granted.”
（还有人在会议录制功能上遇到问题吗？我尝试开始录制，但系统提示 “未获得录制权限”。）
The call keeps dropping when multiple people speak at the same time. Let’s remind everyone to speak one by one, and if the problem persists, we’ll switch to another platform.
（当多个人同时说话时，通话就会中断。我们提醒大家依次发言，如果问题仍然存在，就换个平台。）
I can’t access the online form we need to fill out during the meeting. The page takes a long time to load and eventually shows a “404 error.”
（我无法访问会议期间需要填写的在线表单。页面加载时间很长，最后显示 “404 错误”。）
Could you mute your microphone when you’re typing? The sound of keyboard typing is very loud and distracting, especially when someone is giving a presentation.
（你打字时能把麦克风静音吗？键盘敲击声很大，很容易分散注意力，尤其是有人在做汇报的时候。）
There’s a lot of echo in the meeting, which makes it hard to understand what people are saying. Can everyone check if they have multiple devices connected to the meeting and close the extra ones?
（会议中有很大的回声，让人很难听清别人在说什么。大家能检查一下是否有多个设备连接了会议，并关闭多余的设备吗？）
I’m having trouble connecting to the shared printer to print the meeting materials. The printer is listed in the device list but won’t respond when I send a print command.
（我连接共享打印机打印会议资料时遇到问题。打印机在设备列表中显示，但发送打印命令时没有响应。）
Can you hear me now? I adjusted the audio input settings to use the built-in microphone instead of the external one, which might have been faulty.
（现在能听到我说话了吗？我调整了音频输入设置，使用内置麦克风而不是外部麦克风，外部麦克风可能出故障了。）
I’m experiencing some technical glitches with the team chat software. I can send messages, but I can’t receive any replies from my colleagues.
（我使用团队聊天软件时遇到一些技术故障。我能发送消息，但收不到同事的任何回复。）
Could you share your screen again and highlight the key data in the table? I lost the screen share earlier, and I need to note down those important figures.
（你能再分享屏幕并突出显示表格中的关键数据吗？我之前看不到屏幕共享了，需要记下那些重要数据。）
My video isn’t showing, but I can see everyone else’s. I’ll check the meeting settings to see if my video is disabled by the host.
（我的视频显示不出来，但能看到其他人的。我会检查会议设置，看看主持人是否禁用了我的视频。）
Is anyone else having issues with the internet connection causing the meeting slides to load slowly? I can only see part of the slide content, and the rest is still loading.
（还有人因为网络连接问题导致会议幻灯片加载缓慢吗？我只能看到部分幻灯片内容，其余部分还在加载中。）
The call keeps dropping, and each time I reconnect, I have to re-enter the meeting password. Let’s save the password in a shared document for easy access if we need to reconnect again.
（通话一直中断，每次重新连接都需要重新输入会议密码。我们把密码保存在共享文档中，以便需要再次连接时方便获取。）
I can’t access the cloud storage where the project design drawings are stored. It prompts that the storage space is full, but I checked and there should be enough space left.
（我无法访问存储项目设计图纸的云存储。提示存储空间已满，但我检查过，应该还有足够的剩余空间。）
Could you mute your microphone if you’re in a noisy environment? Even if you’re not speaking, the background noise is being picked up by the microphone and affecting the meeting.
（如果你在嘈杂环境中，请把麦克风静音好吗？即使你不说话，背景噪音也会被麦克风收录，影响会议。）
There’s a lot of static in the audio, making it hard to hear the details of the discussion. Can everyone check their audio devices and reconnect if necessary to fix the issue?
（音频中有很多静电噪音，很难听清讨论的细节。大家能检查一下自己的音频设备，必要时重新连接以解决问题吗？）
核心要点强调
This is a crucial point that we need to remember.
（这是我们需要牢记的一个关键点。）
Let me emphasize the importance of [factor].
（我想强调一下 [因素] 的重要性。）
The key takeaway here is [point].
（这里的关键要点是 [内容]。）
We must pay close attention to [aspect].
（我们必须密切关注 [方面]。）
This issue requires our immediate attention.
（这个问题需要我们立即关注。）
It’s essential that we [action].
（我们必须 [行动]。）
I want to highlight the significance of [topic].
（我想强调一下 [主题] 的重要性。）
Don’t overlook the impact of [factor].
（不要忽视 [因素] 的影响。）
This is a make-or-break situation.
（这是一个成败攸关的情况。）
We should focus on the most critical aspects.
（我们应该关注最关键的方面。）
This is a crucial point that we need to remember: the customer’s demand for on-time delivery is non-negotiable, and any delay will lead to the cancellation of the order.
（这是我们需要牢记的一个关键点：客户对按时交货的要求没有商量余地，任何延误都将导致订单取消。）
Let me emphasize the importance of product quality. In the current competitive market, only high-quality products can help us retain customers and gain a competitive edge.
（我想强调一下产品质量的重要性。在当前竞争激烈的市场中，只有高质量的产品才能帮助我们留住客户，获得竞争优势。）
The key takeaway here is that we need to complete the market research within this month. This will lay the foundation for the formulation of next quarter’s marketing strategy.
（这里的关键要点是，我们需要在本月内完成市场调研。这将为下季度营销策略的制定奠定基础。）
We must pay close attention to the supplier’s delivery schedule. If they fail to deliver the raw materials on time again, we’ll have to find an alternative supplier immediately.
（我们必须密切关注供应商的交货日程。如果他们再次未能按时交付原材料，我们将不得不立即寻找备选供应商。）
This issue of data security requires our immediate attention. Recent cyber threats have increased, and we need to upgrade our protection measures within a week.
（数据安全这个问题需要我们立即关注。近期网络威胁增多，我们需要在一周内升级保护措施。）
It’s essential that we establish a clear communication mechanism between departments. This will avoid misunderstandings and improve work efficiency significantly.
（我们必须在部门之间建立清晰的沟通机制。这将避免误解，并显著提高工作效率。）
I want to highlight the significance of the new project for the company’s long-term development. Its success will open up a new market for us and increase our annual revenue by 30%.
（我想强调一下这个新项目对公司长期发展的重要性。它的成功将为我们开拓新市场，并使年收入增加 30%。）
Don’t overlook the impact of employee satisfaction on work performance. Low satisfaction will lead to high turnover and a decline in productivity, which will affect the company’s operations.
（不要忽视员工满意度对工作绩效的影响。满意度低会导致高流失率和生产率下降，进而影响公司运营。）
This is a make-or-break situation for our sales team. If we can’t meet the quarterly sales target, we’ll lose the opportunity to cooperate with the large client.
（对我们销售团队来说，这是一个成败攸关的情况。如果无法完成季度销售目标，我们将失去与这家大客户合作的机会。）
We should focus on the most critical aspects of the project, such as the core technology development and the final product testing. These two links directly determine the project’s success.
（我们应该关注项目最关键的方面，比如核心技术研发和最终产品测试。这两个环节直接决定项目的成败。）
This is a crucial point that we need to remember: all expenses must be approved in advance, and any unapproved expenditure will not be reimbursed.
（这是我们需要牢记的一个关键点：所有费用都必须提前审批，未经审批的支出将不予报销。）
Let me emphasize the importance of following the project timeline. Each milestone is linked to the next phase of work, and a delay in one milestone will affect the entire project schedule.
（我想强调一下遵守项目时间线的重要性。每个里程碑都与下一阶段的工作相关联，一个里程碑的延误将影响整个项目进度。）
The key takeaway here is that we need to adjust our marketing strategy according to the changes in consumer preferences. Traditional promotional methods are no longer effective in attracting young customers.
（这里的关键要点是，我们需要根据消费者偏好的变化调整营销策略。传统的推广方式在吸引年轻客户方面已不再有效。）
We must pay close attention to the feedback from the first batch of users of the new product. Their opinions will help us identify product defects and make improvements in a timely manner.
（我们必须密切关注新产品首批用户的反馈。他们的意见将帮助我们发现产品缺陷，并及时进行改进。）
This issue of the production line breakdown requires our immediate attention. The factory has stopped production, and we need to send technicians to repair it within two hours.
（生产线故障这个问题需要我们立即关注。工厂已经停产，我们需要在两小时内派技术人员去维修。）
It’s essential that we train employees on the new office software. Without proper training, employees will not be able to use the software proficiently, which will affect work efficiency.
（我们必须对员工进行新办公软件的培训。没有适当的培训，员工无法熟练使用软件，这将影响工作效率。）
I want to highlight the significance of building a good corporate culture. A positive culture can enhance employee loyalty and cohesion, which is crucial for the company’s long-term development.
（我想强调一下建设良好企业文化的重要性。积极的文化能增强员工的忠诚度和凝聚力，这对公司长期发展至关重要。）
Don’t overlook the impact of the new policy on our business. The policy will increase our tax burden, so we need to adjust our financial plan to cope with it.
（不要忽视新政策对我们业务的影响。该政策将增加我们的税务负担，因此我们需要调整财务计划以应对。）
This is a make-or-break situation for the R&D team. If we can’t develop the new technology within the specified time, our competitors will take the lead in the market.
（对研发团队来说，这是一个成败攸关的情况。如果无法在规定时间内研发出这项新技术，我们的竞争对手将在市场中抢占先机。）
We should focus on the most critical aspects of the customer service process, such as response time and problem-solving rate. These directly affect customer satisfaction and retention.
（我们应该关注客户服务流程最关键的方面，比如响应时间和问题解决率。这些直接影响客户满意度和留存率。）
This is a crucial point that we need to remember: when negotiating with the client, we must not compromise on the core terms of the contract, such as the payment method and liability clause.
（这是我们需要牢记的一个关键点：与客户谈判时，在合同核心条款上绝不能妥协，比如付款方式和责任条款。）
Let me emphasize the importance of collecting accurate market data. Inaccurate data will lead to wrong decisions in product development and marketing, resulting in huge losses.
（我想强调一下收集准确市场数据的重要性。数据不准确将导致产品研发和营销方面的错误决策，造成巨大损失。）
The key takeaway here is that we need to establish a risk prevention mechanism for the project. Identifying potential risks in advance and formulating response strategies can avoid unexpected losses.
（这里的关键要点是，我们需要为项目建立风险防范机制。提前识别潜在风险并制定应对策略，可以避免意外损失。）
We must pay close attention to the work progress of the key team members. If any of them encounter difficulties, we need to provide support immediately to ensure the project proceeds smoothly.
（我们必须密切关注核心团队成员的工作进展。如果他们中任何人遇到困难，我们需要立即提供支持，确保项目顺利推进。）
This issue of the customer’s complaint about product quality requires our immediate attention. We need to investigate the cause and provide a solution to the customer within 24 hours.
（客户投诉产品质量这个问题需要我们立即关注。我们需要调查原因，并在 24 小时内为客户提供解决方案。）
It’s essential that we clarify the job responsibilities of each team member. Unclear responsibilities will lead to work overlap or omissions, affecting the team’s work efficiency.
（我们必须明确每个团队成员的工作职责。职责不明确会导致工作重叠或遗漏，影响团队工作效率。）
I want to highlight the significance of the team-building activity. This activity will help improve communication and collaboration between team members, especially after the new employees joined.
（我想强调一下这次团建活动的重要性。这次活动将有助于改善团队成员之间的沟通与协作，尤其是在新员工加入之后。）
Don’t overlook the impact of the office environment on employee productivity. A comfortable and tidy environment can boost employees’ work enthusiasm and efficiency.
（不要忽视办公环境对员工工作效率的影响。舒适整洁的环境能提高员工的工作积极性和效率。）
This is a make-or-break situation for the company’s market expansion. If we fail to enter the southern market this year, we’ll lose the opportunity to grow for the next three years.
（对公司的市场拓展来说，这是一个成败攸关的情况。如果今年无法进入南方市场，我们将失去未来三年的发展机会。）
We should focus on the most critical aspects of the budget plan, such as the allocation of funds to R&D and marketing. These two areas are key to the company’s future growth.
（我们应该关注预算计划最关键的方面，比如研发和营销的资金分配。这两个领域是公司未来发展的关键。）
This is a crucial point that we need to remember: all meeting minutes must be sent to the participants within 24 hours, and the action items must be clearly marked with responsible persons and deadlines.
（这是我们需要牢记的一个关键点：所有会议纪要必须在 24 小时内发送给参会人员，行动事项必须明确标注负责人和截止日期。）
Let me emphasize the importance of complying with the company’s rules and regulations. Any violation, such as disclosing confidential information, will result in severe disciplinary action.
（我想强调一下遵守公司规章制度的重要性。任何违规行为，如泄露机密信息，都将受到严厉的纪律处分。）
The key takeaway here is that we need to improve the after-sales service system. A 完善的 after-sales service can enhance customer loyalty and promote repeat purchases.
（这里的关键要点是，我们需要完善售后服务体系。完善的售后服务能提高客户忠诚度，促进复购。）
We must pay close attention to the changes in the industry competition pattern. New competitors may emerge at any time, and we need to adjust our strategy to maintain our market position.
（我们必须密切关注行业竞争格局的变化。新的竞争对手随时可能出现，我们需要调整策略以维持市场地位。）
This issue of the delay in the patent application requires our immediate attention. The patent is crucial for the protection of our new product, and we need to follow up with the patent office closely.
（专利申请延误这个问题需要我们立即关注。该专利对我们新产品的保护至关重要，我们需要与专利局密切跟进。）
It’s essential that we conduct a comprehensive review of the project before its launch. This review will help us identify any potential problems and ensure the project is launched successfully.
（我们必须在项目启动前对其进行全面审查。这次审查将帮助我们发现任何潜在问题，确保项目成功启动。）
I want to highlight the significance of the employee training program. This program will help employees improve their professional skills, which is essential for the company’s development in the new business field.
（我想强调一下这个员工培训计划的重要性。该计划将帮助员工提升专业技能，这对公司在新业务领域的发展至关重要。）
Don’t overlook the impact of the supply chain disruption on our production. We need to establish a backup supply chain to cope with unexpected situations such as natural disasters.
（不要忽视供应链中断对我们生产的影响。我们需要建立备用供应链，以应对自然灾害等突发情况。）
This is a make-or-break situation for the product launch. If the launch fails to attract enough attention, the product will be eliminated from the market soon.
（对产品推出来说，这是一个成败攸关的情况。如果推出未能吸引足够关注，该产品很快就会被市场淘汰。）
We should focus on the most critical aspects of the performance evaluation system, such as the fairness of evaluation indicators and the connection between evaluation results and incentives. These determine whether the system can motivate employees.
（我们应该关注绩效考核制度最关键的方面，比如考核指标的公平性和考核结果与激励的关联性。这些决定了该制度能否激励员工。）
参与积极性调动
Everyone’s input is valuable. Please share your thoughts.
（每个人的意见都很重要，请分享你们的想法。）
Don't be shy. Even small ideas can make a big difference.
（别害羞，哪怕是小想法也能发挥大作用。）
Newcomers, we'd love to hear your fresh perspectives.
（新同事们，我们很想听听你们的新见解。）
Remember, there are no bad ideas in this brainstorming session.
（记住，在这场头脑风暴中没有糟糕的想法。）
Let's make this a collaborative discussion. Speak up!
（让我们共同参与讨论，大胆发言吧！）
Your experience in this area could provide crucial insights.
（你在这方面的经验能带来关键见解，分享一下吧。）
We're all here to learn from each other, so share freely.
（我们聚在这里是为了相互学习，所以畅所欲言吧。）
If you're unsure but have a hunch, it's worth mentioning.
（如果你不太确定但有预感，也值得一说。）
Let's build on each other's ideas and create something great.
（让我们在彼此的想法上更进一步，共创佳绩。）
The quieter voices in the room are just as important.
（会议室里安静的人，他们的意见同样重要。）
Everyone’s input is valuable when discussing the customer service improvement plan. Please share your thoughts on how to shorten the response time or improve the problem-solving rate.
（讨论客户服务改进计划时，每个人的意见都很重要。请分享你们关于如何缩短响应时间或提高问题解决率的想法。）
Don't be shy about sharing your suggestions for the team-building activity. Even small ideas, like choosing a specific outdoor game, can make the activity more interesting and effective.
（分享对团建活动的建议时别害羞。哪怕是像选择某个户外游戏这样的小想法，也能让活动更有趣、更有效。）
Newcomers, we'd love to hear your fresh perspectives on the company's marketing strategy. You may notice problems or opportunities that we, as old employees, have overlooked.
（新同事们，我们很想听听你们对公司营销策略的新见解。你们可能会发现我们这些老员工忽略的问题或机遇。）
Remember, there are no bad ideas in this brainstorming session for the new product design. Even a seemingly unrealistic idea could inspire a great solution.
（记住，在这场新产品设计的头脑风暴中没有糟糕的想法。即使是看似不切实际的想法，也可能激发出很棒的解决方案。）
Let's make this a collaborative discussion about the project budget allocation. Speak up about which departments you think need more funds and why—your opinion matters.
（让我们共同参与关于项目预算分配的讨论。大胆说出你认为哪些部门需要更多资金以及原因 —— 你的意见很重要。）
Your experience in handling supplier disputes could provide crucial insights for our current negotiation with the difficult supplier. Please share your past experiences and strategies.
（你在处理供应商纠纷方面的经验，能为我们当前与这家难缠供应商的谈判带来关键见解。请分享你过去的经验和策略。）
We're all here to learn from each other when discussing the data analysis methods. So share freely about the tools or techniques you've used that have worked well.
（讨论数据分析方法时，我们聚在这里是为了相互学习。所以畅所欲言，分享你用过且效果不错的工具或技巧吧。）
If you're unsure but have a hunch about why the sales volume is declining, it's worth mentioning. Even a tentative thought could lead us to the root cause of the problem.
（如果你不太确定但对销量下降的原因有预感，也值得一说。即使是初步的想法，也可能引导我们找到问题的根本原因。）
Let's build on each other's ideas when planning the annual meeting. If someone suggests a theme, others can add ideas about the agenda or activities to create something great together.
（规划年会时，让我们在彼此的想法上更进一步。如果有人提出主题，其他人可以补充关于议程或活动的想法，共同打造一场精彩的年会。）
The quieter voices in the room are just as important when we're discussing the employee welfare policy. Don't hesitate to share your needs or suggestions—we want to hear from everyone.
（我们讨论员工福利政策时，会议室里安静的人的意见同样重要。不要犹豫，分享你的需求或建议 —— 我们想听到每个人的声音。）
Everyone’s input is valuable for the office layout optimization. Whether you prefer an open workspace or a private office, please share your thoughts to make the new layout more comfortable.
（对于办公布局优化，每个人的意见都很重要。无论你喜欢开放式办公空间还是独立办公室，请分享你的想法，让新布局更舒适。）
Don't be shy to express your concerns about the new project timeline. Even a small worry, like a potential delay in a specific task, could help us adjust the plan in advance.
（表达对新项目时间线的担忧时别害羞。即使是对某个具体任务可能延误这样的小顾虑，也能帮助我们提前调整计划。）
Newcomers, we'd love to hear your fresh perspectives on the team's work process. You may have experience with more efficient methods from your previous job that we can adopt.
（新同事们，我们很想听听你们对团队工作流程的新见解。你们可能从之前的工作中获得过更高效的方法，我们可以借鉴。）
Remember, there are no bad ideas in this brainstorming session for cost-saving measures. Even a suggestion to reduce office supply waste is worth discussing.
（记住，在这场成本节约措施的头脑风暴中没有糟糕的想法。即使是减少办公用品浪费这样的建议，也值得讨论。）
Let's make this a collaborative discussion about the customer complaint handling process. Speak up about any problems you've encountered when dealing with complaints—your feedback will help us improve.
（让我们共同参与关于客户投诉处理流程的讨论。大胆说出你处理投诉时遇到的任何问题 —— 你的反馈将帮助我们改进。）
Your experience in training new employees could provide crucial insights for the revised training plan. Please share what content or methods you think are most effective.
（你在培训新员工方面的经验，能为修订后的培训计划带来关键见解。请分享你认为最有效的内容或方法。）
We're all here to learn from each other when talking about the market competition. So share freely about what you've observed about our competitors' strengths or weaknesses.
（谈论市场竞争时，我们聚在这里是为了相互学习。所以畅所欲言，分享你观察到的竞争对手的优势或劣势吧。）
If you're unsure but have a hunch about which promotional channel will work best for the new product, it's worth mentioning. We can test your idea on a small scale first.
（如果你不太确定但对哪个推广渠道最适合新产品有预感，也值得一说。我们可以先小规模测试你的想法。）
Let's build on each other's ideas when designing the product packaging. If someone suggests a color scheme, others can add ideas about the shape or logo to make the packaging more attractive.
（设计产品包装时，让我们在彼此的想法上更进一步。如果有人提出配色方案，其他人可以补充关于形状或 logo 的想法，让包装更具吸引力。）
The quieter voices in the room are just as important when we're discussing the company's development goals. Even if you only have a small suggestion about a specific goal, please share it—every input counts.
（我们讨论公司发展目标时，会议室里安静的人的意见同样重要。即使你对某个具体目标只有一个小建议，也请分享出来 —— 每一条意见都有价值。）
Everyone’s input is valuable for the supplier selection process. Please share your criteria for evaluating suppliers, such as delivery speed or product quality, to help us make the right choice.
（对于供应商选择流程，每个人的意见都很重要。请分享你评估供应商的标准，比如交货速度或产品质量，帮助我们做出正确选择。）
Don't be shy to ask questions or share your confusion about the new software. Even a simple question could help other colleagues who have the same confusion.
（对新软件有疑问或困惑时别害羞，大胆提问或分享。即使是简单的问题，也能帮助其他有同样困惑的同事。）

（记住，在这场团队绩效提升的头脑风暴中没有糟糕的想法。即使是召开每周进度会议这样的建议，也值得考虑。）

（让我们共同参与关于新产品发布活动的讨论。大胆说出你对活动的任何创意想法，比如邀请名人嘉宾或举办抽奖活动。）

（讨论数据安全风险时，我们聚在这里是为了相互学习。所以畅所欲言，分享你在日常工作中发现的任何潜在风险吧。）

（如果你不太确定但对如何改善团队沟通有预感，也值得一说。即使是像使用某个特定聊天群这样的小改变，也可能带来不同。）

（让我们共同参与关于绩效考核指标的讨论。大胆说出你认为衡量工作绩效最重要的指标是什么。）

（你处理紧急情况的经验，能为我们的危机管理计划带来关键见解。请分享你过去如何应对紧急情况，以及我们能从中吸取什么教训。）

（谈论产品质量改进时，我们聚在这里是为了相互学习。所以畅所欲言，分享你发现的任何质量问题以及你认为如何解决这些问题吧。）
